# Desktop D2: Playback Engine — Implementation Plan

> **For agentic workers:** REQUIRED SUB-SKILL: Use superpowers:subagent-driven-development (recommended) or superpowers:executing-plans to implement this plan task-by-task. Steps use checkbox (`- [ ]`) syntax for tracking.

**Goal:** Give Musicfy desktop a real playback engine: bundled libVLC behind `PlaybackEngine`, a 127.0.0.1 `StreamProxy` that adds googlevideo's headers, a `PlayerController` with queue/shuffle/repeat/retry/persistence/history, a 10-band EQ with loudness normalisation, QuickJS cipher/n-transform, opt-in JCEF BotGuard, OS media controls, single instance + `musicfy://`, and an offline download engine. Android stays byte-for-byte identical in behaviour.

**Architecture:** New plain-JVM modules `core:playback` (shared queue logic over `MediaMetadata`), `desktop:player` (engine, proxy, controller, EQ, persistence), `desktop:js` (QuickJS `JsRuntime`), `desktop:web` (JCEF installer + `BotGuardRuntime`), `desktop:integration` (single instance, deep links, media session) and `desktop:downloads`, wired with Koin in the existing `desktopApp`. Everything the UI, OS media keys and deep links do goes through `PlayerController.dispatch(PlayerCommand)` (the Ritme seam, spec K18). D2 ships only debug UI; the real screens are D3.

**Tech Stack:** Kotlin 2.3.10 / JDK 21, Gradle 9.3.1, Compose Multiplatform 1.10.1 (desktopApp), Koin 4.2.2, vlcj 4.8.3 + libVLC 3.0.23, OkHttp 5.3.2 (+ mockwebserver3 5.3.2), quickjs-kt 1.0.3, jcefmaven 146.0.10 (CEF 146), JNA 5.14.0, JMTC 0.0.3, Swift (macOS helper), kotlinx-coroutines 1.10.2, kotlinx-serialization-json 1.9.0, Room 2.8.4 (existing desktop DB), JUnit 4.13.2.

Spec: `docs/superpowers/specs/2026-11-22-desktop-d2-d4-design.md` (sections "Keputusan", "Koreksi terhadap spec D1", §0–§2, §5.0, §5.1, §6–§8). Builds on D1b (`docs/superpowers/plans/2026-11-22-desktop-d1b.md`, Tasks 3–9): `desktopApp` with `Main.kt`, `DesktopModule.kt`, `SearchController.kt`, `SearchScreen.kt` and the tests `DesktopModuleTest`/`SearchControllerTest` must exist before Task 6. **Do not start D2 before `./gradlew :desktopApp:run` from D1b works** (spec header).

## Plan-level deviations from the spec

Each one was checked against the code; the reason is given so a reviewer can reject it.

1. **Queue file is a desktop DTO, not `PersistQueue`.** `PersistQueue` is `java.io.Serializable`, not `@kotlinx.serialization.Serializable`, and its `MediaMetadata` holds `LocalDateTime`s. Making it kotlinx-serializable would touch the class Android restores with `ObjectInputStream`. Desktop writes its own `@Serializable QueueSnapshot` to `localData/queue.json` (Task 9). A restored queue is a plain list: radio continuations are not persisted (Android's `QueueData` is not reproduced).
2. **History follows Android, not the spec's "≥ 30 s atau ≥ 50%" / `PlayCountEntity`.** `MusicService.onPlaybackStatsReady` writes an `Event` and `incrementTotalPlayTime` once `totalPlayTimeMs ≥ HistoryDuration × 1000` (default 30 s) unless `PauseListenHistoryKey`. `DatabaseDao.incrementPlayCount` is never called anywhere in `app/`. Desktop does exactly what Android does (the spec itself says "ambang sama dengan Android").
3. **Normalisation follows Android, not "clamp(-loudnessDb, -12, +6)".** `MusicService.setupLoudnessEnhancer` uses `loudnessDb ?: perceptualLoudnessDb`, gain `-loudness` dB clamped to `MIN_GAIN_MB = -1500` / `MAX_GAIN_MB = 300`, i.e. **[-15, +3] dB**. The on/off switch is the existing shared `AudioNormalizationKey` (default `true`), not a new `NormalizeVolumeKey`.
4. **E2 reference values come from V8 (Node 24) running the same EJS solver over the same player.js**, not from an Android device. Android's `EjsSolverWebView` runs exactly these three files in Chromium's V8; agents have no device. A device cross-check is listed as optional manual work. Desktop uses EJS for **both** n and sig, so `QuickJsRuntime.loadCipher` ignores the regex-extracted `sigInfo`/`nFuncInfo`.
5. **E2 timing:** "init ≤ 3 s" is applied to the **warm** init (EJS output cached per player.js hash). The cold preprocess (once per player version) is recorded and must be ≤ 10 s, which is the spec's own threshold for abandoning QuickJS.
6. **E3/E4 run from `gradle run`/probe mains, not from a jpackage app.** jpackage arrives in D4.1, whose smoke test re-runs them packaged. E1's HTTP leg uses a range-capable `MockWebServer`; the proxy leg is covered by Task 5's integration test.
7. **`EngineEvent.Advanced(uri)`** is added: when a preloaded player takes over, the controller must move to the next index without calling `load()`.
8. **`ResolverPlatform.forcedStreamClient: String? = null`** is added to `core:resolver` for `--force-client`/`-Dmusicfy.resolver.forceClient` (spec E3, D2.5). Android passes nothing, so the fallback loop is unchanged; existing `YTPlayerUtilsTest` cases prove it.
9. **`fetch-libvlc` is one bash script** (Git Bash on Windows runners), not `.sh` + `.ps1`.
10. **`DesktopBotGuardRuntime.isSupported` is always `true`.** `PoTokenGenerator` reads `isSupported` once (`by lazy`), so a runtime that starts "unsupported" could never use CEF installed later. Without CEF, `newSession()` throws `BotGuardUnavailableException`; `PoTokenGenerator` turns that into its 5-minute cooldown and the resolver moves to the next client (the spec's described fallback).
11. **CEF cache paths:** CEF requires `cache_path` inside `root_cache_path`, so they are `localData/cef-data` and `localData/cef-data/profile` (spec said `localData/cef-profile`).
12. **`PlayerCommand` gains `TogglePlayPause`, `SetShuffle`, `SetRepeat`, `SetVolume`;** `SetQueue.startIndex = -1` means "the queue's own current item".
13. **`jvmTests` also runs `:desktop:*:test`** (unit tests only; libVLC tests are a separate `integrationTest` task), so the one shared-test command covers D2.
14. **`--self-test` (spec §6.3) and the optional `-Pnetwork-tests` suite are not in D2.** `--self-test` belongs to D4.2's installer smoke test; `--play`, `e3Probe` and the integration tests cover the same paths now.

## Global Constraints

- Android behaviour must not change. After **every** task: `./gradlew :app:assembleUniversalFossDebug :app:assembleUniversalGmsDebug :app:assembleUniversalFdroidDebug` → `BUILD SUCCESSFUL`.
- Room database version stays **39**. After every task `git status --porcelain -- app/schemas core/database/schemas` prints nothing. D2 adds DAO queries only.
- `core/*` modules: no DI annotations, no desktop or Android APIs (no vlcj, JCEF, JNA, AWT, `android.*`). Spec §1.2 rule 2.
- Only `desktop:player` imports `uk.co.caprica.vlcj.*`. Only `desktop:web` imports `org.cef.*`/`me.friwi.*`. JNA (`com.sun.jna.*`) only in `desktop:player` and `desktop:integration`. `desktop/*` main code does not depend on another `desktop/*` module except `desktop:downloads → desktop:player` and `desktop:integration → desktop:player`. `app/` never depends on `desktop/*`. Spec §1.2.
- Exact versions: vlcj `4.8.3` (→ vlcj-natives 4.8.3, JNA 5.14.0), libVLC `3.0.23`, quickjs-kt-jvm `1.0.3` (built with Kotlin 2.3.10 / coroutines 1.10.2, same as this build; 1.0.6+ needs Kotlin 2.4.10), jcefmaven `146.0.10`, JNA `5.14.0` (`jna-jpms`, `jna-platform-jpms`), JMTC `0.0.3`, mockwebserver3 `5.3.2` (= `okhttp`), kotlinx-coroutines-test `1.10.2` (= `coroutines`). Existing: Koin `4.2.2`, OkHttp `5.3.2`, kotlinx-serialization-json `1.9.0`, Compose Multiplatform `1.10.1`.
- libVLC archives (SHA-256 from get.videolan.org's `.sha256` files): `vlc-3.0.23-arm64.dmg` `fc6fac08d87f538517d44aca0c5e7a244b67c8c4cb589bf478363a7315fd5e0d`, `vlc-3.0.23-intel64.dmg` `ec01530ce69d849dd057fba8876e68ac39bf279dc28de4e9c04e4aec11fc98db`, `vlc-3.0.23-win64.zip` `992d19dbd0b8a7cde9167d2f7780b1ef6f92acc8a71acfa736101a21f35181e1`. Linux uses the system `libvlc5` (not bundled).
- JCEF natives 146.0.10, tag `jcef-d3de827+cef-146.0.10+g8219561+chromium-146.0.7680.179` (SHA-256 computed from Maven Central, SHA-1 matched Central's `.sha1`): `macosx-arm64` 135870260 B `b9c8ad6809372db9b6597911b7d3f12f0f6b9b23ef1506261b5518f78332642b`; `macosx-amd64` 145034271 B `4c05aae6c80841bf763a9d0dcdc3c3332af57abe0ab76091fb7bb9ef128e1bad`; `windows-amd64` 162120546 B `e9a2d4ef3c33b372c24655ee4cdaf9ef23fe2de5f7e57410576569977299c4b4`; `linux-amd64` 147903463 B `b089370c3bf8f853183ec822817e1b58348d90d1236bb6a26e921f513449dac2`. CEF is never downloaded without the user pressing Install.
- `StreamProxy` binds `127.0.0.1` only, random port, random 128-bit token in the path; wrong token → 404.
- Chunk sizes: proxy **512 KiB**, downloads **4 MiB**. Downloads: at most **2** in parallel.
- Retry: **3** per song, **1 s** apart; a song that exhausts them is skipped and stays "recently failed" for **5 min**.
- Preload the next track at **duration − 15 s**; hand-off target **≤ 300 ms** (logged as `gapless hand-off: N ms`).
- EQ: **10** bands at 60, 170, 310, 600 Hz, 1, 3, 6, 12, 14, 16 kHz; band and preamp range **−20…+20 dB**; libVLC's **18** presets.
- New desktop-only preference keys live in `desktopApp/.../DesktopPreferenceKeys.kt` with a `desktop_` prefix; nothing is added to `core:settings` (its golden file must not change).
- Tests that load libVLC carry `@Category(NeedsLibVlc::class)` and run only in `:desktop:player:integrationTest`. Nothing that needs CEF or a display is a unit test; those are probe mains.
- Never commit `desktopApp/resources/*/vlc/`, `desktopApp/resources/*/native/`, `build/`, or the CEF download.
- Never use bare `git stash`. Commit with two `-m` flags, the second being exactly `Co-Authored-By: Claude Opus 5.5 <noreply@anthropic.com>`.

### Task 1: Spike E1 (time-box 2 days) — bundled libVLC plays opus/webm and m4a over HTTP on 3 platforms; `localData`; `fetch-libvlc`

**Decision this task makes (spec §5.0 E1):**

| | Criterion |
|---|---|
| **GO** | On macOS arm64 (local), macOS x64 (`macos-15-intel`) and Windows x64 (`windows-latest`): `BundledVlcSmokeTest` passes, i.e. `silence-5s.webm` (opus) and `silence-5s.m4a` (AAC) served over HTTP with Range support reach `finished`, a seek to 2.5 s (50 %) lands, `file://` playback works, and `nativeLibraryPath()` is the bundled copy. No VLC installed on any machine. |
| **NO-GO** | Any of the three platforms still fails after **2 working days** of fixing (plugin list, discovery, options). |
| **On NO-GO** | Stop executing this plan. Record the failure in the spec section added in Step 12 and ask for a revised plan with `MpvPlaybackEngine` (libmpv + own JNA binding, spec K1 fallback). Tasks 2–4 and 7 are engine-independent and stay valid; Tasks 5 and 10 are rewritten. |
| **Partial** | macOS arm64 passes but the CI legs have not run because the branch is not pushed: continue with Tasks 2–4 (they do not use libVLC) and **do not start Task 5** until the user has pushed and the `engine` job is green on `macos-15-intel` and `windows-latest`. |

```kotlin
package com.example.musicfy.core.platform

import org.junit.Assert.assertEquals

class DesktopAppDirsTest {
    private val home = File("/home/u")

        val dirs = DesktopAppDirs.resolve(

        assertEquals(File("C:/Users/u/AppData/Local/Musicfy/download"), dirs.downloads)

    @Test
    fun windowsFallsBackToHomeWhenEnvMissing() {
        val dirs = DesktopAppDirs.resolve("Windows 10", emptyMap(), home)
        assertEquals(File("/home/u/AppData/Roaming/Musicfy"), dirs.data)
        assertEquals(File("/home/u/AppData/Local/Musicfy/Cache"), dirs.cache)
        assertEquals(File("/home/u/AppData/Local/Musicfy"), dirs.localData)
    }

    @Test

        val dirs = DesktopAppDirs.resolve("Mac OS X", emptyMap(), File("/Users/u"))
        assertEquals(File("/Users/u/Library/Application Support/Musicfy"), dirs.data)
        assertEquals(File("/Users/u/Library/Caches/Musicfy"), dirs.cache)
        assertEquals(dirs.data, dirs.localData)
        assertEquals(File("/Users/u/Library/Application Support/Musicfy/download"), dirs.downloads)

    @Test

        val dirs = DesktopAppDirs.resolve("Linux", emptyMap(), home)
        assertEquals(File("/home/u/.local/share/musicfy"), dirs.data)
        assertEquals(File("/home/u/.cache/musicfy"), dirs.cache)

    @Test

        val dirs = DesktopAppDirs.resolve(
            osName = "Linux",
            env = mapOf("XDG_DATA_HOME" to "/data", "XDG_CACHE_HOME" to "  "),
            home = home,
        )
        assertEquals(File("/data/musicfy"), dirs.data)
        assertEquals(File("/home/u/.cache/musicfy"), dirs.cache)
    }
}
```

- [ ] **Step 3: Implement `localData`**

`core/platform/src/main/kotlin/com/example/musicfy/core/platform/DesktopAppDirs.kt`:
```kotlin
package com.example.musicfy.core.platform

import java.io.File

class DesktopAppDirs private constructor(
    override val data: File,
    override val cache: File,
    /**
     * Large, machine-local files: downloads, the browser component, the persisted queue. Windows keeps
     * them in %LOCALAPPDATA%\Musicfy so a roaming profile does not sync them; elsewhere this is [data].
     */
    val localData: File,
) : AppDirs {
    override val downloads: File
        get() = localData.resolve("download")

    companion object {
        private const val APP_NAME = "Musicfy"
        private const val LINUX_DIR = "musicfy"

        fun forCurrentOs(): DesktopAppDirs = resolve(
            osName = System.getProperty("os.name").orEmpty(),
            env = System.getenv(),
            home = File(System.getProperty("user.home")),
        )

        fun resolve(osName: String, env: Map<String, String>, home: File): DesktopAppDirs {
            fun envDir(name: String): File? = env[name]?.takeIf { it.isNotBlank() }?.let(::File)
            val os = osName.lowercase()
            return when {
                os.startsWith("windows") -> {
                    val local = (envDir("LOCALAPPDATA") ?: home.resolve("AppData/Local")).resolve(APP_NAME)
                    DesktopAppDirs(
                        data = (envDir("APPDATA") ?: home.resolve("AppData/Roaming")).resolve(APP_NAME),
                        cache = local.resolve("Cache"),
                        localData = local,
                    )
                }
                os.startsWith("mac") -> {
                    val data = home.resolve("Library/Application Support").resolve(APP_NAME)
                    DesktopAppDirs(data = data, cache = home.resolve("Library/Caches").resolve(APP_NAME), localData = data)
                }
                else -> {
                    val data = (envDir("XDG_DATA_HOME") ?: home.resolve(".local/share")).resolve(LINUX_DIR)
                    DesktopAppDirs(
                        data = data,
                        cache = (envDir("XDG_CACHE_HOME") ?: home.resolve(".cache")).resolve(LINUX_DIR),
                        localData = data,
                    )
                }
            }
        }
    }
}
```

```kotlin
plugins {
    id("kotlin")
}

kotlin {
    jvmToolchain(21)
}

dependencies {
    api(project(":core:platform"))
    implementation(libs.vlcj)
    testImplementation(libs.junit)
    testImplementation(libs.mockwebserver3)
}

// desktopApp/resources/<os-arch>/vlc, filled by scripts/desktop/fetch-libvlc.sh (Linux: system libvlc)
val osArch: String = run {
    val os = System.getProperty("os.name").lowercase()
    val arch = if (System.getProperty("os.arch").lowercase() in setOf("aarch64", "arm64")) "arm64" else "x64"
    when {
        os.startsWith("mac") -> "macos-$arch"
        os.startsWith("windows") -> "windows-$arch"
        else -> "linux-$arch"
    }
}
val bundledVlcDir = rootProject.layout.projectDirectory.dir("desktopApp/resources/$osArch/vlc").asFile
val libVlcCategory = "com.example.musicfy.desktop.player.testing.NeedsLibVlc"
val testSourceSet = the<SourceSetContainer>()["test"]

tasks.test {
    useJUnit { excludeCategories(libVlcCategory) }
}

val integrationTest by tasks.registering(Test::class) {
    description = "Tests that load libVLC. Run scripts/desktop/fetch-libvlc.sh first (Linux: apt install libvlc-dev vlc-plugin-base)."
    group = "verification"
    testClassesDirs = testSourceSet.output.classesDirs
    classpath = testSourceSet.runtimeClasspath
    useJUnit { includeCategories(libVlcCategory) }
    systemProperty("musicfy.vlc.dir", bundledVlcDir.absolutePath)
    systemProperty("musicfy.vlc.audible", providers.gradleProperty("audible").getOrElse("false"))
    testLogging {
        events("passed", "failed", "skipped")
        showStandardStreams = true
    }
    shouldRunAfter(tasks.test)
}
```

`desktop/player/vlc-plugins.txt`:
```text
# libVLC plugins bundled with Musicfy desktop (read by scripts/desktop/fetch-libvlc.sh).
# Format: [mac:|win:]<category>/<name>[?]
#   <name> is the file lib<name>_plugin.dylib / lib<name>_plugin.dll; '?' marks it optional.
#   macOS builds keep every plugin in one folder; the category only matters on Windows.
# Before release, D4.1 audits each entry's licence (spec §4.8): GPL-2.0-only plugins must go.

# input: the proxy is plain http on 127.0.0.1; downloaded songs are local files
access/http
access/https?
access/filesystem
stream_filter/prefetch?
stream_filter/cache_read?

# containers: webm (itag 249/250/251) and m4a (139/140)
demux/mkv
demux/mp4
demux/es
packetizer/packetizer_mpeg4audio?
packetizer/packetizer_copy?

# decoders
codec/opus
codec/faad?
codec/avcodec

# output; adummy is --aout=dummy for tests and CI
mac:audio_output/auhal
win:audio_output/mmdevice
win:audio_output/directsound?
audio_output/adummy

# EQ (+ normalisation preamp), resampling, format and channel conversion, mixing
audio_filter/equalizer
audio_filter/soxr?
audio_filter/ugly_resampler
audio_filter/speex_resampler?
audio_filter/samplerate?
audio_filter/audio_format
audio_filter/trivial_channel_mixer
audio_filter/simple_channel_mixer?
audio_filter/remap?
audio_mixer/float_mixer
audio_mixer/integer_mixer

misc/logger?
```
`scripts/desktop/fetch-libvlc.sh`:
```bash
#!/usr/bin/env bash
# Downloads the official VLC 3.0.23 build for one platform, checks its pinned SHA-256, and copies
# libVLC plus the plugins listed in desktop/player/vlc-plugins.txt into
# desktopApp/resources/<os-arch>/vlc (git-ignored; Compose packages it via appResourcesRootDir).
# Usage: scripts/desktop/fetch-libvlc.sh [macos-arm64|macos-x64|windows-x64]   (default: this machine)
set -euo pipefail

VLC_VERSION=3.0.23
ROOT="$(cd "$(dirname "$0")/../.." && pwd)"
PLUGINS_FILE="$ROOT/desktop/player/vlc-plugins.txt"
CACHE="$ROOT/build/libvlc-cache"

detect() {
  case "$(uname -s)" in
    Darwin) if [ "$(uname -m)" = "arm64" ]; then echo macos-arm64; else echo macos-x64; fi ;;
    MINGW*|MSYS*|CYGWIN*) echo windows-x64 ;;
    *) echo linux ;;
  esac
}
TARGET="${1:-$(detect)}"

case "$TARGET" in
  macos-arm64) FILE="vlc-$VLC_VERSION-arm64.dmg";    DIR=macosx; OSKEY=mac; SHA=fc6fac08d87f538517d44aca0c5e7a244b67c8c4cb589bf478363a7315fd5e0d ;;
  macos-x64)   FILE="vlc-$VLC_VERSION-intel64.dmg";  DIR=macosx; OSKEY=mac; SHA=ec01530ce69d849dd057fba8876e68ac39bf279dc28de4e9c04e4aec11fc98db ;;
  windows-x64) FILE="vlc-$VLC_VERSION-win64.zip";    DIR=win64;  OSKEY=win; SHA=992d19dbd0b8a7cde9167d2f7780b1ef6f92acc8a71acfa736101a21f35181e1 ;;
  *) echo "fetch-libvlc: nothing to bundle for '$TARGET' (Linux uses the system libvlc5 package)"; exit 0 ;;
esac

OUT="$ROOT/desktopApp/resources/$TARGET/vlc"
mkdir -p "$CACHE"
ARCHIVE="$CACHE/$FILE"
if [ ! -f "$ARCHIVE" ]; then
  curl -fL --retry 3 -o "$ARCHIVE.tmp" "https://get.videolan.org/vlc/$VLC_VERSION/$DIR/$FILE"
  mv "$ARCHIVE.tmp" "$ARCHIVE"
fi

sha256() { if command -v sha256sum >/dev/null 2>&1; then sha256sum "$1" | cut -d' ' -f1; else shasum -a 256 "$1" | cut -d' ' -f1; fi; }
ACTUAL="$(sha256 "$ARCHIVE")"
if [ "$ACTUAL" != "$SHA" ]; then
  echo "fetch-libvlc: SHA-256 mismatch for $FILE: expected $SHA, got $ACTUAL" >&2
  rm -f "$ARCHIVE"
  exit 1
fi

WORK="$(mktemp -d)"
MOUNT=""
cleanup() { if [ -n "$MOUNT" ]; then hdiutil detach -quiet "$MOUNT" || true; fi; rm -rf "$WORK"; }
trap cleanup EXIT

rm -rf "$OUT"
mkdir -p "$OUT/plugins"

if [ "$OSKEY" = mac ]; then
  MOUNT="$WORK/mnt"
  mkdir -p "$MOUNT"
  hdiutil attach -quiet -nobrowse -readonly -mountpoint "$MOUNT" "$ARCHIVE"
  SRC="$MOUNT/VLC.app/Contents/MacOS"
  mkdir -p "$OUT/lib"
  cp -L "$SRC/lib/libvlc.dylib" "$SRC/lib/libvlccore.dylib" "$OUT/lib/"
  plugin_src() { echo "$SRC/plugins/lib$2_plugin.dylib"; }
  plugin_dst() { echo "$OUT/plugins/lib$2_plugin.dylib"; }
else
  if command -v unzip >/dev/null 2>&1; then unzip -q "$ARCHIVE" -d "$WORK"; else 7z x -y -o"$WORK" "$ARCHIVE" >/dev/null; fi
  SRC="$WORK/vlc-$VLC_VERSION"
  cp "$SRC/libvlc.dll" "$SRC/libvlccore.dll" "$OUT/"
  plugin_src() { echo "$SRC/plugins/$1/lib$2_plugin.dll"; }
  plugin_dst() { echo "$OUT/plugins/$1/lib$2_plugin.dll"; }
fi

missing=0
while IFS= read -r raw || [ -n "$raw" ]; do
  line="${raw%%#*}"
  line="$(printf '%s' "$line" | tr -d '[:space:]')"
  [ -z "$line" ] && continue
  only=""
  case "$line" in mac:*) only=mac; line="${line#mac:}" ;; win:*) only=win; line="${line#win:}" ;; esac
  if [ -n "$only" ] && [ "$only" != "$OSKEY" ]; then continue; fi
  optional=0
  case "$line" in *\?) optional=1; line="${line%\?}" ;; esac
  category="${line%%/*}"
  name="${line#*/}"
  src="$(plugin_src "$category" "$name")"
  dst="$(plugin_dst "$category" "$name")"
  if [ -f "$src" ]; then
    mkdir -p "$(dirname "$dst")"
    cp -L "$src" "$dst"
  elif [ "$optional" = 1 ]; then
    echo "fetch-libvlc: optional plugin $category/$name is not in this build; skipped"
  else
    echo "fetch-libvlc: required plugin $category/$name is missing from $FILE ($src)" >&2
    missing=1
  fi
done < "$PLUGINS_FILE"
[ "$missing" = 0 ] || exit 1

echo "$VLC_VERSION" > "$OUT/VERSION"
echo "fetch-libvlc: libVLC $VLC_VERSION for $TARGET in $OUT ($(du -sh "$OUT" | cut -f1))"
```

If the script reports a **required** plugin missing: mount the DMG (`hdiutil attach -readonly build/libvlc-cache/vlc-3.0.23-arm64.dmg`) and `ls /Volumes/VLC*/VLC.app/Contents/MacOS/plugins | grep <name>`. Fix the name, or mark it `?` if VLC 3.0.23 simply does not ship it, then re-run. This is part of the time-box.

- [ ] **Step 7: Discovery and options**

`desktop/player/src/main/kotlin/com/example/musicfy/desktop/player/vlc/VlcNatives.kt`:
```kotlin
package com.example.musicfy.desktop.player.vlc

import uk.co.caprica.vlcj.factory.discovery.NativeDiscovery
import uk.co.caprica.vlcj.factory.discovery.strategy.LinuxNativeDiscoveryStrategy
import uk.co.caprica.vlcj.factory.discovery.strategy.NativeDiscoveryStrategy
import java.io.File

/** Finds libVLC: the copy bundled in the app's resources on macOS/Windows, the system one on Linux. */
object VlcNatives {
    const val DIR_PROPERTY = "musicfy.vlc.dir"
    private const val COMPOSE_RESOURCES_PROPERTY = "compose.application.resources.dir"

    /** `-Dmusicfy.vlc.dir` (tests), else `<compose resources dir>/vlc` (`gradle run` and the packaged app). */
    fun bundledDir(properties: (String) -> String? = System::getProperty): File? {
        properties(DIR_PROPERTY)?.let { return File(it).takeIf(File::isDirectory) }
        return properties(COMPOSE_RESOURCES_PROPERTY)?.let { File(it, "vlc") }?.takeIf(File::isDirectory)
    }

    fun discovery(bundled: File? = bundledDir()): NativeDiscovery {
        val strategies = buildList<NativeDiscoveryStrategy> {
            bundled?.let { add(BundledVlcDiscoveryStrategy(it)) }
            if (System.getProperty("os.name").orEmpty().lowercase().startsWith("linux")) add(LinuxNativeDiscoveryStrategy())
        }
        return NativeDiscovery(*strategies.toTypedArray())
    }
}
```
`desktop/player/src/main/kotlin/com/example/musicfy/desktop/player/vlc/BundledVlcDiscoveryStrategy.kt`:
```kotlin
package com.example.musicfy.desktop.player.vlc

import com.sun.jna.NativeLibrary
import uk.co.caprica.vlcj.binding.lib.LibC
import uk.co.caprica.vlcj.binding.support.runtime.RuntimeUtil
import uk.co.caprica.vlcj.factory.discovery.strategy.NativeDiscoveryStrategy
import java.io.File

/**
 * libVLC as fetch-libvlc.sh lays it out: macOS `vlc/lib/libvlc.dylib` + `vlc/plugins/`, Windows
 * `vlc/libvlc.dll` + `vlc/plugins/<category>/`. Once found it does what vlcj's own OS strategies do:
 * preload libvlccore on macOS and point VLC_PLUGIN_PATH at the bundled plugins.
 */
class BundledVlcDiscoveryStrategy(private val root: File) : NativeDiscoveryStrategy {
    private val libDir: File = if (RuntimeUtil.isMac()) root.resolve("lib") else root
    private val libFile: File = libDir.resolve(if (RuntimeUtil.isWindows()) "libvlc.dll" else "libvlc.dylib")

    override fun supported(): Boolean = (RuntimeUtil.isMac() || RuntimeUtil.isWindows()) && libFile.isFile

    override fun discover(): String? = libDir.absolutePath.takeIf { libFile.isFile }

    override fun onFound(path: String): Boolean {
        if (RuntimeUtil.isMac()) {
            NativeLibrary.addSearchPath(RuntimeUtil.getLibVlcCoreLibraryName(), path)
            NativeLibrary.getInstance(RuntimeUtil.getLibVlcCoreLibraryName())
        }
        return true
    }

    override fun onSetPluginPath(path: String): Boolean {
        val plugins = root.resolve("plugins").absolutePath
        return if (RuntimeUtil.isWindows()) {
            LibC.INSTANCE._putenv("VLC_PLUGIN_PATH=$plugins") == 0
        } else {
            LibC.INSTANCE.setenv("VLC_PLUGIN_PATH", plugins, 1) == 0
        }
    }
}
```
`desktop/player/src/main/kotlin/com/example/musicfy/desktop/player/vlc/VlcOptions.kt`:
```kotlin
package com.example.musicfy.desktop.player.vlc

/**
 * libVLC instance arguments (spec §2.3). [silent] adds `--aout=dummy` (tests, CI). The soxr resampler
 * falls back to libVLC's default when the plugin is absent.
 */
fun vlcArgs(silent: Boolean, verbose: Boolean = false): List<String> = buildList {
    add("--no-video")
    add("--intf=dummy")
    add("--no-lua")
    add("--network-caching=1500")
    add("--file-caching=300")
    add("--audio-resampler=soxr")
    if (silent) add("--aout=dummy")
    add(if (verbose) "--verbose=2" else "--verbose=0")
}
```

`desktop/player/src/test/kotlin/com/example/musicfy/desktop/player/testing/RangeFileServer.kt`:
```kotlin
package com.example.musicfy.desktop.player.testing

import mockwebserver3.Dispatcher
import mockwebserver3.MockResponse
import mockwebserver3.MockWebServer
import mockwebserver3.RecordedRequest
import okhttp3.Headers
import okio.Buffer
import java.util.concurrent.ConcurrentHashMap
import java.util.concurrent.CopyOnWriteArrayList

/** Serves byte arrays at /<name> with HTTP Range support, like googlevideo, and records every request. */
class RangeFileServer(
    private val files: Map<String, ByteArray>,
    private val mimeType: String = "application/octet-stream",
) : AutoCloseable {
    private val server = MockWebServer()

    /** The Range header of each request, in order (null = none). */
    val ranges: MutableList<String?> = CopyOnWriteArrayList()
    val requestHeaders: MutableList<Headers> = CopyOnWriteArrayList()

    /** Request number (0-based) → status to answer instead of the bytes, e.g. 403 for an expired url. */
    val failures: MutableMap<Int, Int> = ConcurrentHashMap()

    init {
        server.dispatcher = object : Dispatcher() {
            override fun dispatch(request: RecordedRequest): MockResponse {
                val number = ranges.size
                ranges += request.headers["Range"]
                requestHeaders += request.headers
                failures[number]?.let { return MockResponse.Builder().code(it).build() }
                val bytes = files[request.url.encodedPath.removePrefix("/")]
                    ?: return MockResponse.Builder().code(404).build()
                val range = request.headers["Range"]?.let(::parseRange)
                    ?: return MockResponse.Builder().code(200).setHeader("Content-Type", mimeType).body(Buffer().write(bytes)).build()
                val start = range.first
                if (start >= bytes.size) {
                    return MockResponse.Builder().code(416).setHeader("Content-Range", "bytes */${bytes.size}").build()
                }
                val end = minOf(range.second ?: (bytes.size - 1L), bytes.size - 1L)
                return MockResponse.Builder()
                    .code(206)
                    .setHeader("Content-Type", mimeType)
                    .setHeader("Content-Range", "bytes $start-$end/${bytes.size}")
                    .body(Buffer().write(bytes, start.toInt(), (end - start + 1).toInt()))
                    .build()
            }
        }
        server.start()
    }

    fun url(name: String): String = server.url("/$name").toString()

    override fun close() = server.close()

    private fun parseRange(header: String): Pair<Long, Long?>? {
        val match = Regex("""bytes=(\d+)-(\d*)""").matchEntire(header.trim()) ?: return null
        return match.groupValues[1].toLong() to match.groupValues[2].takeIf { it.isNotEmpty() }?.toLong()
    }
}
```
`desktop/player/src/test/kotlin/com/example/musicfy/desktop/player/vlc/VlcOptionsTest.kt`:
```kotlin
package com.example.musicfy.desktop.player.vlc

import org.junit.Assert.assertEquals
import org.junit.Assert.assertFalse
import org.junit.Assert.assertTrue
import org.junit.Test
import java.io.File

class VlcOptionsTest {
    @Test
    fun silentAddsTheDummyOutputAndNeverAddsHttpHeaders() {
        assertTrue("--aout=dummy" in vlcArgs(silent = true))
        assertFalse("--aout=dummy" in vlcArgs(silent = false))
        assertTrue(vlcArgs(silent = false).containsAll(listOf("--no-video", "--network-caching=1500", "--file-caching=300")))
        assertTrue(vlcArgs(silent = false).none { it.startsWith(":http") })
    }

    @Test
    fun bundledDirPrefersTheTestPropertyThenComposeResources() {
        val dir = File(System.getProperty("java.io.tmpdir"))
        val resources = mapOf("compose.application.resources.dir" to dir.parent, VlcNatives.DIR_PROPERTY to dir.path)
        assertEquals(dir, VlcNatives.bundledDir(resources::get))
        assertEquals(null, VlcNatives.bundledDir(mapOf(VlcNatives.DIR_PROPERTY to "/definitely/missing")::get))
        assertEquals(null, VlcNatives.bundledDir(emptyMap<String, String>()::get))
    }
}
```

`desktop/player/src/test/kotlin/com/example/musicfy/desktop/player/vlc/BundledVlcSmokeTest.kt`:
```kotlin
package com.example.musicfy.desktop.player.vlc

import com.example.musicfy.desktop.player.testing.NeedsLibVlc
import com.example.musicfy.desktop.player.testing.RangeFileServer
import org.junit.AfterClass
import org.junit.Assert.assertFalse
import org.junit.Assert.assertTrue
import org.junit.BeforeClass
import org.junit.Rule
import org.junit.Test
import org.junit.experimental.categories.Category
import org.junit.rules.TemporaryFolder
import uk.co.caprica.vlcj.binding.support.runtime.RuntimeUtil
import uk.co.caprica.vlcj.factory.MediaPlayerFactory
import uk.co.caprica.vlcj.player.base.MediaPlayer
import uk.co.caprica.vlcj.player.base.MediaPlayerEventAdapter
import java.io.File
import java.util.concurrent.CountDownLatch
import java.util.concurrent.TimeUnit.SECONDS
import java.util.concurrent.atomic.AtomicBoolean
import java.util.concurrent.atomic.AtomicLong

/** Experiment E1 (spec §5.0): raw vlcj against the bundled libVLC, no Musicfy engine code involved. */
@Category(NeedsLibVlc::class)
class BundledVlcSmokeTest {
    @get:Rule
    val tmp = TemporaryFolder()

    companion object {
        private lateinit var factory: MediaPlayerFactory

        @BeforeClass
        @JvmStatic
        fun loadLibVlc() {
            val audible = System.getProperty("musicfy.vlc.audible") == "true"
            factory = try {
                MediaPlayerFactory(VlcNatives.discovery(), *vlcArgs(silent = !audible, verbose = true).toTypedArray())
            } catch (t: Throwable) {
                throw AssertionError("libVLC did not load (bundled dir: ${VlcNatives.bundledDir()}). Run scripts/desktop/fetch-libvlc.sh", t)
            }
            println("E1: libVLC loaded from ${factory.nativeLibraryPath()}")
        }

        @AfterClass
        @JvmStatic
        fun release() {
            if (::factory.isInitialized) factory.release()
        }
    }

    private fun fixture(name: String): ByteArray =
        BundledVlcSmokeTest::class.java.getResourceAsStream("/fixtures/$name")!!.readBytes()

    @Test
    fun theBundledCopyIsLoadedOnMacAndWindows() {
        if (!RuntimeUtil.isMac() && !RuntimeUtil.isWindows()) return
        val bundled = VlcNatives.bundledDir()!!.canonicalPath
        assertTrue("loaded ${factory.nativeLibraryPath()}, expected under $bundled", File(factory.nativeLibraryPath()).canonicalPath.startsWith(bundled))
    }

    @Test
    fun opusWebmOverHttpPlaysSeeksAndEnds() = playsSeeksAndEnds("silence-5s.webm")

    @Test
    fun aacM4aOverHttpPlaysSeeksAndEnds() = playsSeeksAndEnds("silence-5s.m4a")

    @Test
    fun localFilePlaysToTheEnd() {
        val file = tmp.newFile("song.webm").apply { writeBytes(fixture("silence-5s.webm")) }
        val result = play(file.toPath().toUri().toString(), seekToMs = null)
        assertFalse("libVLC reported an error for file://", result.errored)
        assertTrue("file:// never finished", result.finished)
    }

    private fun playsSeeksAndEnds(name: String) {
        RangeFileServer(mapOf(name to fixture(name))).use { http ->
            val result = play(http.url(name), seekToMs = 2_500)
            assertFalse("libVLC reported an error for $name", result.errored)
            assertTrue("$name: length ${result.lengthMs} ms, expected about 5000", result.lengthMs in 4_500..5_500)
            assertTrue("$name: seek to 2500 ms did not land (max time ${result.maxTimeMs} ms)", result.seekLanded)
            assertTrue("$name never finished", result.finished)
        }
    }

    private class PlayResult(val errored: Boolean, val finished: Boolean, val lengthMs: Long, val maxTimeMs: Long, val seekLanded: Boolean)

    private fun play(mrl: String, seekToMs: Long?): PlayResult {
        val player = factory.mediaPlayers().newMediaPlayer()
        val playing = CountDownLatch(1)
        val finished = CountDownLatch(1)
        val errored = AtomicBoolean(false)
        val maxTime = AtomicLong(0)
        val length = AtomicLong(0)
        player.events().addMediaPlayerEventListener(object : MediaPlayerEventAdapter() {
            override fun playing(mediaPlayer: MediaPlayer) = playing.countDown()
            override fun finished(mediaPlayer: MediaPlayer) = finished.countDown()
            override fun error(mediaPlayer: MediaPlayer) {
                errored.set(true)
                playing.countDown()
                finished.countDown()
            }
            override fun timeChanged(mediaPlayer: MediaPlayer, newTime: Long) {
                maxTime.accumulateAndGet(newTime, ::maxOf)
            }
            override fun lengthChanged(mediaPlayer: MediaPlayer, newLength: Long) = length.set(newLength)
        })
        try {
            assertTrue("play() refused $mrl", player.media().play(mrl))
            assertTrue("never started playing $mrl", playing.await(15, SECONDS))
            var seekLanded = seekToMs == null
            if (seekToMs != null && !errored.get()) {
                player.controls().setTime(seekToMs)
                val deadline = System.currentTimeMillis() + 4_000
                while (System.currentTimeMillis() < deadline && maxTime.get() < seekToMs - 100) Thread.sleep(50)
                seekLanded = maxTime.get() >= seekToMs - 100
            }
            val done = finished.await(20, SECONDS)
            return PlayResult(errored.get(), done && !errored.get(), length.get(), maxTime.get(), seekLanded)
        } finally {
            player.controls().stop()
            player.release()
        }
    }
}
```

Run: `./gradlew :desktop:player:test`
Expected: PASS (2 tests in `VlcOptionsTest`; the smoke test is excluded).

Run: `ls -d /Applications/VLC.app 2>/dev/null; ./gradlew --console=plain :desktop:player:integrationTest`
Expected: `ls` prints nothing (no system VLC). `E1: libVLC loaded from …/desktopApp/resources/macos-arm64/vlc/lib`, then 4 tests PASSED.

Manual listen (optional, needs a person): `./gradlew :desktop:player:integrationTest -Paudible=true` plays the silent files through the real output (`auhal`) without errors.

If a test fails, rerun and read libVLC's `--verbose=2` lines in the output: `no suitable demux module` / `no suitable decoder module` / `no suitable audio output module` name the missing plugin. Add it to `vlc-plugins.txt`, rerun `scripts/desktop/fetch-libvlc.sh`, repeat. Record every change for Step 12.

```yaml
name: Desktop

on:
  push:
    branches: [ "main", "feature/desktop-*" ]
    paths: [ "core/**", "desktop/**", "desktopApp/**", "providers/**", "scripts/desktop/**", "gradle/**", "*.gradle.kts", ".github/workflows/desktop.yml" ]
  pull_request:
    paths: [ "core/**", "desktop/**", "desktopApp/**", "providers/**", "scripts/desktop/**", "gradle/**", "*.gradle.kts", ".github/workflows/desktop.yml" ]
  workflow_dispatch:

concurrency:
  group: desktop-${{ github.ref }}
  cancel-in-progress: true

jobs:
  engine:
    name: libVLC engine (${{ matrix.os }})
    runs-on: ${{ matrix.os }}
    strategy:
      fail-fast: false
      matrix:
        os: [ macos-15, macos-15-intel, windows-latest, ubuntu-latest ]
    defaults:
      run:
        shell: bash
    steps:
      - uses: actions/checkout@v6
        with:
          submodules: recursive

      - name: Set up JDK 21
        uses: actions/setup-java@v5
        with:
          java-version: 21
          distribution: "temurin"

      - name: Set Up Gradle
        uses: gradle/actions/setup-gradle@v5
        with:
          cache-read-only: ${{ github.ref != 'refs/heads/main' }}

      - name: libVLC from the system (Linux)
        if: runner.os == 'Linux'
        run: sudo apt-get update && sudo apt-get install -y libvlc-dev vlc-plugin-base

      - name: Bundled libVLC 3.0.23
        if: runner.os != 'Linux'
        run: scripts/desktop/fetch-libvlc.sh

      - name: Engine tests (headless, --aout=dummy)
        run: ./gradlew --console=plain :desktop:player:test :desktop:player:integrationTest

      - uses: actions/upload-artifact@v6
        if: failure()
        with:
          name: engine-reports-${{ matrix.os }}
          path: desktop/player/build/reports/tests
```

### Task 2: Spike E2 — QuickJS `JsRuntime` (EJS n + sig) and the `--force-client` seam

**Decision this task makes (spec §5.0 E2):**

| | Criterion |
|---|---|
| **GO** | `QuickJsRuntimeTest` matches the V8 reference for **20/20 n** and **20/20 sig** challenges over the recorded player.js, cold and warm; on the Mac with `-Pe2.enforceTiming=true`: warm init ≤ 3 s, ≤ 50 ms per call, cold init ≤ 10 s. |
| **NO-GO (wrong output or QuickJS cannot run EJS)** | Keep `UnsupportedJsRuntime` in Task 6, record the result, and ask for a follow-up task: `GraalJsRuntime` with `org.graalvm.polyglot:polyglot` + `js` (version resolved then, interpreter mode). Tasks 3–14 do not depend on JS. |
| **NO-GO (warm init > 10 s)** | Same, but the follow-up is a JCEF-based `JsRuntime` on top of Task 3's `CefRuntime`. |

- [ ] **Step 5: Record the E2 reference with V8**

`scripts/desktop/ejs-reference.mjs`:
```js
#!/usr/bin/env node
// Experiment E2 reference: yt-dlp's EJS solver (app/src/main/assets/solver, the files Android's
// EjsSolverWebView runs) executed by V8 over the current YouTube player.js, for 20 n and 20
// signature challenges. Writes (commit both):
//   desktop/js/src/test/resources/ejs/player.js.gz
//   desktop/js/src/test/resources/ejs/expected.json
// Usage: node scripts/desktop/ejs-reference.mjs
import { readFileSync, writeFileSync, mkdirSync } from 'node:fs';
import { gzipSync } from 'node:zlib';
import { fileURLToPath } from 'node:url';
import path from 'node:path';
import vm from 'node:vm';

const root = path.resolve(path.dirname(fileURLToPath(import.meta.url)), '../..');
const solverDir = path.join(root, 'app/src/main/assets/solver');
const outDir = path.join(root, 'desktop/js/src/test/resources/ejs');

async function get(url) {
  const res = await fetch(url, { headers: { 'User-Agent': 'Mozilla/5.0' } });
  if (!res.ok) throw new Error(`${url} -> HTTP ${res.status}`);
  return res.text();
}

// Same endpoints and hash regex as core:resolver's PlayerJsFetcher.
const iframe = await get('https://www.youtube.com/iframe_api');
const hash = /\\?\/s\\?\/player\\?\/([a-zA-Z0-9_-]+)\\?\//.exec(iframe)?.[1];
if (!hash) throw new Error('player hash not found in iframe_api');
const player = await get(`https://www.youtube.com/s/player/${hash}/player_ias.vflset/en_GB/base.js`);

// Deterministic challenges shaped like real ones (n: 16-19 chars, sig: 108 chars).
let seed = 0x6d757369;
function rand() {
  seed = (seed + 0x6d2b79f5) | 0;
  let t = Math.imul(seed ^ (seed >>> 15), 1 | seed);
  t = (t + Math.imul(t ^ (t >>> 7), 61 | t)) ^ t;
  return ((t ^ (t >>> 14)) >>> 0) / 4294967296;
}
const alphabet = 'ABCDEFGHIJKLMNOPQRSTUVWXYZabcdefghijklmnopqrstuvwxyz0123456789-_';
const word = (len) => Array.from({ length: len }, () => alphabet[Math.floor(rand() * alphabet.length)]).join('');
function unique(count, make) {
  const set = new Set();
  while (set.size < count) set.add(make());
  return [...set];
}
const nChallenges = unique(20, () => word(16 + Math.floor(rand() * 4)));
const sigChallenges = unique(20, () => word(108));

const ctx = vm.createContext({});
for (const file of ['meriyah.js', 'astring.js', 'yt.solver.core.js']) {
  vm.runInContext(readFileSync(path.join(solverDir, file), 'utf8'), ctx, { filename: file });
}
ctx.__input = {
  type: 'player',
  player,
  requests: [
    { type: 'n', challenges: nChallenges },
    { type: 'sig', challenges: sigChallenges },
  ],
};
const out = vm.runInContext('jsc(__input)', ctx);
if (out.type !== 'result') throw new Error(`EJS: ${out.error}`);
const [n, sig] = out.responses;
for (const r of [n, sig]) if (r.type !== 'result') throw new Error(`EJS request failed: ${r.error}`);

mkdirSync(outDir, { recursive: true });
writeFileSync(path.join(outDir, 'player.js.gz'), gzipSync(player, { level: 9 }));
writeFileSync(
  path.join(outDir, 'expected.json'),
  JSON.stringify({ playerHash: hash, recordedWith: `node ${process.version}`, n: n.data, sig: sig.data }, null, 2) + '\n',
);
console.log(`E2 reference: player ${hash}, ${Object.keys(n.data).length} n + ${Object.keys(sig.data).length} sig -> ${outDir}`);
```
Run: `node scripts/desktop/ejs-reference.mjs && ls -l desktop/js/src/test/resources/ejs`
Expected: `E2 reference: player <hash>, 20 n + 20 sig -> …`; `player.js.gz` (~0.6–0.9 MB) and `expected.json`.

Optional manual cross-check (needs an Android device with this branch's Foss debug build): not required for GO.

`desktop/js/src/test/kotlin/com/example/musicfy/desktop/js/QuickJsRuntimeTest.kt`:
```kotlin
package com.example.musicfy.desktop.js

import com.example.musicfy.core.platform.StdoutLogger
import com.example.musicfy.core.resolver.ResolverPlatform
import com.example.musicfy.core.resolver.ResolverRuntime
import com.example.musicfy.core.resolver.StreamUnplayableException
import com.example.musicfy.core.resolver.UnsupportedBotGuardRuntime
import com.example.musicfy.core.resolver.UriCodec
import com.example.musicfy.utils.sabr.EjsNTransformSolver
import kotlinx.coroutines.runBlocking
import kotlinx.serialization.json.Json
import kotlinx.serialization.json.jsonObject
import kotlinx.serialization.json.jsonPrimitive
import org.junit.Assert.assertEquals
import org.junit.Assert.assertFalse
import org.junit.Assert.assertNotNull
import org.junit.Assert.assertTrue
import org.junit.Rule
import org.junit.Test
import org.junit.rules.TemporaryFolder
import java.io.File
import java.util.zip.GZIPInputStream

/** Experiment E2 (spec §5.0), kept as a regression test with the recorded player.js. */
class QuickJsRuntimeTest {
    @get:Rule
    val tmp = TemporaryFolder()

    private val fixture = EjsFixture.load()
    private val enforceTiming = System.getProperty("musicfy.e2.enforceTiming") == "true"

    @Test
    fun matchesTheV8ReferenceForEveryChallengeColdAndWarm() = runBlocking {
        assertEquals(20, fixture.n.size)
        assertEquals(20, fixture.sig.size)
        val runtime = QuickJsRuntime(tmp.newFolder("ejs"), StdoutLogger)

        val cold = runtime.load(fixture.playerJs)
        val coldCallMs = try {
            assertTrue(cold.cold)
            assertTrue(cold.nFunctionAvailable)
            assertTrue(cold.sigFunctionAvailable)
            assertMatches(cold)
        } finally {
            cold.dispose()
        }

        val warm = runtime.load(fixture.playerJs)
        try {
            assertFalse(warm.cold)
            val warmCallMs = assertMatches(warm)
            println("E2 player ${fixture.hash}: cold init ${cold.initMillis} ms, warm init ${warm.initMillis} ms, " + "per call ${"%.1f".format(coldCallMs)} / ${"%.1f".format(warmCallMs)} ms")
            if (enforceTiming) {
                assertTrue("cold init ${cold.initMillis} ms > 10000", cold.initMillis <= 10_000)
                assertTrue("warm init ${warm.initMillis} ms > 3000", warm.initMillis <= 3_000)
                assertTrue("per call $warmCallMs ms > 50", warmCallMs <= 50.0)
            }
        } finally {
            warm.dispose()
        }
    }

    @Test
    fun brokenPlayerJsFailsAndCachesNothing() = runBlocking {
        val cache = tmp.newFolder("ejs")
        val error = runCatching { QuickJsRuntime(cache, StdoutLogger).load("var notAPlayer = 1;") }.exceptionOrNull()
        assertNotNull(error)
        assertTrue(cache.listFiles().orEmpty().isEmpty())
    }

    @Test
    fun theResolverSeamUsesQuickJsForTheNTransform() = runBlocking {
        val data = tmp.newFolder("data")
        File(data, "cipher_cache").apply { mkdirs() }.let { dir ->
            File(dir, "player_${fixture.hash}.js").writeText(fixture.playerJs)
            File(dir, "current_hash.txt").writeText("${fixture.hash}\n${System.currentTimeMillis()}")
        }
        ResolverRuntime.install(
            ResolverPlatform(
                logger = StdoutLogger,
                releaseLog = { _, _ -> },
                dataDir = data,
                jsRuntime = QuickJsRuntime(tmp.newFolder("ejs-cache"), StdoutLogger),
                botGuardRuntime = UnsupportedBotGuardRuntime,
                playbackError = { StreamUnplayableException(it) },
                onVisitorDataRotated = { },
            ),
        )
        val (input, expected) = fixture.n.entries.first()
        val url = EjsNTransformSolver.transformNParamInUrl("https://rr1.googlevideo.com/videoplayback?id=1&n=${UriCodec.encode(input)}&c=WEB_REMIX")
        assertEquals("https://rr1.googlevideo.com/videoplayback?id=1&n=${UriCodec.encode(expected)}&c=WEB_REMIX", url)
        EjsNTransformSolver.close()
    }

    /** Returns the mean milliseconds per call. */
    private suspend fun assertMatches(session: EjsSession): Double {
        val started = System.nanoTime()
        fixture.n.forEach { (input, expected) -> assertEquals("n($input)", expected, session.transformN(input)) }
        fixture.sig.forEach { (input, expected) -> assertEquals("sig($input)", expected, session.deobfuscateSignature(input)) }
        return (System.nanoTime() - started) / 1e6 / (fixture.n.size + fixture.sig.size)
    }
}

private class EjsFixture(val hash: String, val playerJs: String, val n: Map<String, String>, val sig: Map<String, String>) {
    companion object {
        fun load(): EjsFixture {
            val playerJs = GZIPInputStream(EjsFixture::class.java.getResourceAsStream("/ejs/player.js.gz")!!).bufferedReader().readText()
            val json = Json.parseToJsonElement(EjsFixture::class.java.getResourceAsStream("/ejs/expected.json")!!.bufferedReader().readText()).jsonObject
            fun map(key: String) = json.getValue(key).jsonObject.mapValues { it.value.jsonPrimitive.content }
            return EjsFixture(json.getValue("playerHash").jsonPrimitive.content, playerJs, map("n"), map("sig"))
        }
    }
}
```
Note: `PlayerJsFetcher` treats `cipher_cache/current_hash.txt` (`hash\ntimestamp`) younger than its TTL as fresh, so the third test makes no network call.

`desktop/js/src/main/kotlin/com/example/musicfy/desktop/js/EjsScripts.kt`:
```kotlin
package com.example.musicfy.desktop.js

import kotlinx.serialization.json.JsonPrimitive

/** The EJS solver sources (meriyah, astring, yt.solver.core) and the glue QuickJsRuntime evaluates. */
class EjsScripts(val meriyah: String, val astring: String, val solverCore: String) {
    companion object {
        fun fromClasspath(): EjsScripts {
            fun read(name: String) = EjsScripts::class.java.getResourceAsStream("/ejs/$name")
                ?.bufferedReader()?.use { it.readText() }
                ?: error("EJS asset ejs/$name missing from the classpath")
            return EjsScripts(read("meriyah.js"), read("astring.js"), read("yt.solver.core.js"))
        }

        /** A JS string literal (JSON quoting is valid ES2019+ JS, which QuickJS implements). */
        fun literal(s: String): String = JsonPrimitive(s).toString()

        /** Runs EJS's preprocess pass; evaluates to the preprocessed player source. */
        fun preprocessCall(playerJs: String): String = """
            (function () {
                var out = jsc({ type: 'player', player: ${literal(playerJs)}, requests: [], output_preprocessed: true });
                if (out.type === 'error') throw new Error('EJS preprocess: ' + out.error);
                return out.preprocessed_player;
            })()
        """.trimIndent()

        /** Loads a preprocessed player into `_mf`; evaluates to "nAvailable,sigAvailable". */
        fun initCall(preprocessed: String): String = "_mfInit(${literal(preprocessed)})"

        /** Same contract as EJS's getFromPrepared(): Function('_result', code)(resultObj). */
        const val HELPERS = """
            var _mf = { n: null, sig: null };
            function _mfInit(code) {
                Function('_result', code)(_mf);
                return (typeof _mf.n === 'function') + ',' + (typeof _mf.sig === 'function');
            }
            function _mfCall(kind, value) {
                var f = _mf[kind];
                if (typeof f !== 'function') throw new Error(kind + ' solver not available');
                var r = f(value);
                if (r === undefined || r === null) throw new Error(kind + ' solver returned ' + r);
                return String(r);
            }
        """
    }
}
```
`desktop/js/src/main/kotlin/com/example/musicfy/desktop/js/EjsSession.kt`:
```kotlin
package com.example.musicfy.desktop.js

import com.dokar.quickjs.QuickJs
import com.example.musicfy.core.resolver.CipherSession
import com.example.musicfy.utils.cipher.CipherException
import com.example.musicfy.utils.sabr.SabrException
import kotlinx.coroutines.CoroutineDispatcher
import kotlinx.coroutines.withContext
import java.util.concurrent.ExecutorService

/** One QuickJS context holding one player version's n and sig functions. Single-threaded by construction. */
class EjsSession internal constructor(
    private val js: QuickJs,
    private val dispatcher: CoroutineDispatcher,
    private val executor: ExecutorService,
    override val nFunctionAvailable: Boolean,
    val sigFunctionAvailable: Boolean,
    val initMillis: Long,
    val cold: Boolean,
) : CipherSession {
    override suspend fun transformN(nValue: String): String {
        if (!nFunctionAvailable) throw SabrException("EJS n-transform not available")
        return call("n", nValue)
    }

    override suspend fun deobfuscateSignature(obfuscatedSig: String): String {
        if (!sigFunctionAvailable) throw CipherException("EJS signature function not available")
        return call("sig", obfuscatedSig)
    }

    private suspend fun call(kind: String, value: String): String = withContext(dispatcher) {
        js.evaluate<String>("_mfCall(${EjsScripts.literal(kind)}, ${EjsScripts.literal(value)})", "call.js", false)
    }

    override suspend fun dispose() {
        withContext(dispatcher) { js.close() }
        executor.shutdown()
    }
}
```

```kotlin
package com.example.musicfy.desktop.js

import com.dokar.quickjs.QuickJs
import com.example.musicfy.core.platform.Logger
import com.example.musicfy.core.resolver.CipherSession
import com.example.musicfy.core.resolver.JsRuntime
import com.example.musicfy.core.resolver.NSolverSession
import com.example.musicfy.utils.cipher.FunctionNameExtractor
import kotlinx.coroutines.asCoroutineDispatcher
import kotlinx.coroutines.withContext
import java.io.File
import java.nio.file.Files
import java.nio.file.StandardCopyOption
import java.security.MessageDigest
import java.util.concurrent.Executors

/**
 * Desktop [JsRuntime] (spec K3): QuickJS running yt-dlp's EJS solver, the same solver Android's
 * EjsSolverWebView runs. EJS locates both the n and the signature function itself, so [loadCipher]
 * does not need the regex-extracted names Android's CipherWebView uses.
 *
 * EJS's preprocess pass (meriyah parse + astring print of ~2.5 MB of player.js) is the slow part; its
 * output is cached in [cacheDir] under the SHA-256 of the player source, so it runs once per version.
 */
class QuickJsRuntime(
    private val cacheDir: File,
    private val logger: Logger,
    private val scripts: EjsScripts = EjsScripts.fromClasspath(),
) : JsRuntime {
    override suspend fun loadCipher(
        playerJs: String,
        sigInfo: FunctionNameExtractor.SigFunctionInfo,
        nFuncInfo: FunctionNameExtractor.NFunctionInfo?,
    ): CipherSession = load(playerJs)

    override suspend fun loadNSolver(playerJs: String): NSolverSession = load(playerJs)

    suspend fun load(playerJs: String): EjsSession {
        val started = System.nanoTime()
        // QuickJS checks recursion against the stack of the thread it runs on, and meriyah recurses
        // deeply over player.js: give the context its own thread with a large stack, and only use it there.
        val executor = Executors.newSingleThreadExecutor { r ->
            Thread(null, r, "quickjs-ejs", THREAD_STACK_BYTES).apply { isDaemon = true }
        }
        val dispatcher = executor.asCoroutineDispatcher()
        try {
            return withContext(dispatcher) {
                val js = QuickJs.create(dispatcher)
                js.maxStackSize = JS_STACK_BYTES
                try {
                    val cacheFile = cacheDir.resolve("${sha256(playerJs)}.js")
                    val cold = !cacheFile.isFile
                    val preprocessed = if (cold) {
                        js.evaluate<Any?>(scripts.meriyah, "meriyah.js", false)
                        js.evaluate<Any?>(scripts.astring, "astring.js", false)
                        js.evaluate<Any?>(scripts.solverCore, "yt.solver.core.js", false)
                        js.evaluate<String>(EjsScripts.preprocessCall(playerJs), "preprocess.js", false)
                    } else {
                        cacheFile.readText()
                    }
                    js.evaluate<Any?>(EjsScripts.HELPERS, "helpers.js", false)
                    val flags = js.evaluate<String>(EjsScripts.initCall(preprocessed), "init.js", false).split(',')
                    val n = flags.getOrNull(0) == "true"
                    val sig = flags.getOrNull(1) == "true"
                    if (!n && !sig) error("EJS found neither the n nor the signature function")
                    if (cold) writeAtomically(cacheFile, preprocessed)
                    val millis = (System.nanoTime() - started) / 1_000_000
                    logger.i(TAG, "EJS ready in ${millis}ms (${if (cold) "cold" else "warm"}): n=$n sig=$sig")
                    EjsSession(js, dispatcher, executor, n, sig, millis, cold)
                } catch (t: Throwable) {
                    js.close()
                    throw t
                }
            }
        } catch (t: Throwable) {
            executor.shutdownNow()
            throw t
        }
    }

    private fun writeAtomically(target: File, text: String) {
        cacheDir.mkdirs()
        val tmp = File(cacheDir, "${target.name}.tmp")
        tmp.writeText(text)
        Files.move(tmp.toPath(), target.toPath(), StandardCopyOption.REPLACE_EXISTING, StandardCopyOption.ATOMIC_MOVE)
    }

    private fun sha256(s: String): String =
        MessageDigest.getInstance("SHA-256").digest(s.toByteArray()).joinToString("") { "%02x".format(it) }

    private companion object {
        const val TAG = "QuickJsRuntime"
        const val THREAD_STACK_BYTES = 64L * 1024 * 1024
        const val JS_STACK_BYTES = 48L * 1024 * 1024
    }
}
```

### Task 3: Spike E3 — on-demand JCEF (`CefInstaller`) and an off-screen `BotGuardRuntime`

**Decision this task makes (spec §5.0 E3):**

| | Criterion |
|---|---|
| **GO** | `./gradlew :desktop:web:e3Probe` on macOS arm64 (local), and the `cef-e3` CI job on `macos-15`, `macos-15-intel`, `windows-latest`, each print `E3 PASS`: CEF natives downloaded and verified against the pinned SHA-256, an **off-screen** browser minted a PoToken, the resolver forced to `WEB_REMIX` returned a `pot=` url, and 3 consecutive 400 000-byte ranges (1.2 MB, past the ~786 KB untokened window) came back `206` with full length. |
| **Fallback A** | Off-screen fails but `-Pe3Args=--windowed` passes: set `CefBotGuardRuntime(offscreen = false)` as the default in Task 6 (hidden 1×1 undecorated window), record "E3a". |
| **Fallback B** | Both fail on a platform: record it; desktop ships without PoToken on that platform (Task 6 makes `CompatibilityOffer` hide itself there) and a follow-up plan evaluates JetBrains Runtime + JCEF (spec K4 fallback). Tasks 4–14 continue either way. |

```kotlin
package com.example.musicfy.desktop.web

import com.example.musicfy.core.platform.StdoutLogger
import kotlinx.coroutines.runBlocking
import mockwebserver3.MockResponse
import mockwebserver3.MockWebServer
import okhttp3.OkHttpClient
import okio.Buffer
import org.junit.After
import org.junit.Assert.assertEquals
import org.junit.Assert.assertFalse
import org.junit.Assert.assertTrue
import org.junit.Before
import org.junit.Rule
import org.junit.Test
import org.junit.rules.TemporaryFolder
import java.security.MessageDigest

class CefInstallerTest {
    @get:Rule
    val tmp = TemporaryFolder()

    private val server = MockWebServer()
    private val bytes = ByteArray(200_000) { (it * 31).toByte() }
    private val sha = MessageDigest.getInstance("SHA-256").digest(bytes).joinToString("") { "%02x".format(it) }

    @Before
    fun start() = server.start()

    @After
    fun stop() = server.close()

    private fun installer(natives: CefArtifacts.Natives) =
        CefInstaller(tmp.root, OkHttpClient(), StdoutLogger, natives = natives, urlFor = { server.url("/natives.jar").toString() })

    @Test
    fun downloadIsVerifiedAndMovedIntoPlace() = runBlocking {
        server.enqueue(MockResponse.Builder().code(200).body(Buffer().write(bytes)).build())
        val installer = installer(CefArtifacts.Natives("test-platform", bytes.size.toLong(), sha))

        val jar = installer.downloadVerified()

        assertTrue(jar.name.startsWith("jcef-natives-test-platform-"))
        assertTrue(jar.readBytes().contentEquals(bytes))
        assertFalse(java.io.File(jar.path + ".part").exists())
        assertEquals(CefInstallState.Downloading(1f), installer.state.value)
        assertFalse(installer.isInstalled())
    }

    @Test
    fun wrongHashIsRejectedAndNothingIsKept() = runBlocking {
        server.enqueue(MockResponse.Builder().code(200).body(Buffer().write(bytes)).build())
        val installer = installer(CefArtifacts.Natives("test-platform", bytes.size.toLong(), "0".repeat(64)))

        val error = runCatching { installer.downloadVerified() }.exceptionOrNull()

        assertTrue("got $error", error?.message.orEmpty().contains("SHA-256 mismatch"))
        assertTrue(installer.versionDir.resolve("download").listFiles().orEmpty().isEmpty())
    }

    @Test
    fun wrongSizeIsRejected() = runBlocking {
        server.enqueue(MockResponse.Builder().code(200).body(Buffer().write(bytes)).build())
        val installer = installer(CefArtifacts.Natives("test-platform", bytes.size + 1L, sha))
        val error = runCatching { installer.downloadVerified() }.exceptionOrNull()
        assertTrue("got $error", error?.message.orEmpty().contains("expected ${bytes.size + 1} bytes"))
    }

    @Test
    fun everyPinnedPlatformHasAMavenUrlAndA64CharHash() {
        assertEquals(setOf("macosx-arm64", "macosx-amd64", "windows-amd64", "linux-amd64"), CefArtifacts.natives.keys)
        CefArtifacts.natives.values.forEach {
            assertEquals(64, it.sha256.length)
            assertTrue(CefArtifacts.mavenUrl(it).startsWith("https://repo1.maven.org/maven2/me/friwi/jcef-natives-${it.platform}/"))
        }
    }
}
```

```kotlin
        var offers = 0
        val runtime = DesktopBotGuardRuntime(installed = { false }, cef = { cef }, onUnavailable = { offers++ })
        assertTrue("PoTokenGenerator reads isSupported once, so it must stay true", runtime.isSupported)
        val error = runCatching { runtime.newSession() }.exceptionOrNull()
        assertTrue(error is BotGuardUnavailableException)
        assertEquals(1, offers)

    @Test

        val runtime = DesktopBotGuardRuntime(installed = { true }, cef = { cef }, onUnavailable = { error("not expected") })
        assertSame(session, runtime.newSession())
    }
}
```

```kotlin
package com.example.musicfy.desktop.web

import me.friwi.jcefmaven.EnumPlatform
import java.net.URLEncoder

/** The JCEF natives desktop installs on demand (spec K4). Sizes and SHA-256 pinned from Maven Central. */
object CefArtifacts {
    const val VERSION = "146.0.10"
    const val TAG = "jcef-d3de827+cef-146.0.10+g8219561+chromium-146.0.7680.179"

    data class Natives(val platform: String, val sizeBytes: Long, val sha256: String)

    val natives: Map<String, Natives> = listOf(
        Natives("macosx-arm64", 135_870_260, "b9c8ad6809372db9b6597911b7d3f12f0f6b9b23ef1506261b5518f78332642b"),
        Natives("macosx-amd64", 145_034_271, "4c05aae6c80841bf763a9d0dcdc3c3332af57abe0ab76091fb7bb9ef128e1bad"),
        Natives("windows-amd64", 162_120_546, "e9a2d4ef3c33b372c24655ee4cdaf9ef23fe2de5f7e57410576569977299c4b4"),
        Natives("linux-amd64", 147_903_463, "b089370c3bf8f853183ec822817e1b58348d90d1236bb6a26e921f513449dac2"),
    ).associateBy { it.platform }

    /** Null on platforms we do not pin (e.g. Windows arm64, which runs the x64 build under emulation anyway). */
    fun forCurrentPlatform(): Natives? = runCatching { natives[EnumPlatform.getCurrentPlatform().identifier] }.getOrNull()

    fun mavenUrl(n: Natives): String {
        val tag = URLEncoder.encode(TAG, Charsets.UTF_8)
        return "https://repo1.maven.org/maven2/me/friwi/jcef-natives-${n.platform}/$tag/jcef-natives-${n.platform}-$tag.jar"
    }
}
```

`desktop/web/src/main/kotlin/com/example/musicfy/desktop/web/CefInstaller.kt`:
```kotlin
package com.example.musicfy.desktop.web

import com.example.musicfy.core.platform.Logger
import com.sun.net.httpserver.HttpServer
import kotlinx.coroutines.CancellationException
import kotlinx.coroutines.Dispatchers
import kotlinx.coroutines.ensureActive
import kotlinx.coroutines.flow.MutableStateFlow
import kotlinx.coroutines.flow.StateFlow
import kotlinx.coroutines.flow.asStateFlow
import kotlinx.coroutines.withContext
import me.friwi.jcefmaven.CefAppBuilder
import okhttp3.OkHttpClient
import okhttp3.Request
import java.io.File
import java.io.IOException
import java.net.InetAddress
import java.net.InetSocketAddress
import java.nio.file.Files
import java.nio.file.StandardCopyOption
import java.security.MessageDigest

sealed interface CefInstallState {
    data object NotInstalled : CefInstallState
    data class Downloading(val fraction: Float) : CefInstallState
    data object Extracting : CefInstallState
    data object Installed : CefInstallState
    data class Failed(val message: String) : CefInstallState
}

/**
 * Installs the JCEF natives into `localData/cef/<version>/bundle` when the user asks (spec §2.5 #3).
 * We download and verify the jar ourselves (pinned size + SHA-256), then let jcefmaven unpack it by
 * serving the verified file as its only "mirror" on 127.0.0.1: jcefmaven casts each mirror connection
 * to HttpURLConnection, so a file: url would not work, and its own extractor sets the helper apps'
 * permissions correctly.
 */
class CefInstaller(
    localData: File,
    private val http: OkHttpClient,
    private val logger: Logger,
    private val natives: CefArtifacts.Natives? = CefArtifacts.forCurrentPlatform(),
    private val urlFor: (CefArtifacts.Natives) -> String = CefArtifacts::mavenUrl,
) {
    val versionDir: File = localData.resolve("cef").resolve(CefArtifacts.VERSION)
    val installDir: File = versionDir.resolve("bundle")
    private val downloadDir = versionDir.resolve("download")
    private val _state = MutableStateFlow<CefInstallState>(if (isInstalled()) CefInstallState.Installed else CefInstallState.NotInstalled)
    val state: StateFlow<CefInstallState> = _state.asStateFlow()

    val isSupportedPlatform: Boolean get() = natives != null

    fun isInstalled(): Boolean = installDir.resolve("install.lock").isFile

    /** Cancelling the calling coroutine leaves nothing half-installed. */
    suspend fun install() {
        if (isInstalled()) {
            _state.value = CefInstallState.Installed
            return
        }
        try {
            val jar = downloadVerified()
            _state.value = CefInstallState.Extracting
            unpackWithJcefmaven(jar)
            check(isInstalled()) { "jcefmaven finished without writing install.lock" }
            downloadDir.deleteRecursively()
            _state.value = CefInstallState.Installed
            logger.i(TAG, "JCEF ${CefArtifacts.VERSION} installed in $installDir")
        } catch (e: CancellationException) {
            downloadDir.deleteRecursively()
            installDir.deleteRecursively()
            _state.value = CefInstallState.NotInstalled
            throw e
        } catch (e: Exception) {
            logger.w(TAG, "JCEF install failed", e)
            _state.value = CefInstallState.Failed(e.message ?: e.javaClass.simpleName)
            throw e
        }
    }

    internal suspend fun downloadVerified(): File = withContext(Dispatchers.IO) {
        val n = natives ?: throw UnsupportedOperationException("No pinned JCEF natives for this platform")
        downloadDir.mkdirs()
        val target = downloadDir.resolve("jcef-natives-${n.platform}-${CefArtifacts.TAG}.jar")
        val part = File(target.path + ".part")
        val digest = MessageDigest.getInstance("SHA-256")
        var read = 0L
        try {
            http.newCall(Request.Builder().url(urlFor(n)).build()).execute().use { response ->
                if (!response.isSuccessful) throw IOException("HTTP ${response.code} for ${urlFor(n)}")
                response.body.byteStream().use { input ->
                    part.outputStream().use { out ->
                        val buffer = ByteArray(64 * 1024)
                        while (true) {
                            ensureActive()
                            val k = input.read(buffer)
                            if (k < 0) break
                            out.write(buffer, 0, k)
                            digest.update(buffer, 0, k)
                            read += k
                            _state.value = CefInstallState.Downloading((read.toFloat() / n.sizeBytes).coerceAtMost(1f))
                        }
                    }
                }
            }
            if (read != n.sizeBytes) throw IOException("JCEF natives: expected ${n.sizeBytes} bytes, got $read")
            val sha = digest.digest().joinToString("") { "%02x".format(it) }
            if (sha != n.sha256) throw IOException("JCEF natives: SHA-256 mismatch (expected ${n.sha256}, got $sha)")
            Files.move(part.toPath(), target.toPath(), StandardCopyOption.REPLACE_EXISTING, StandardCopyOption.ATOMIC_MOVE)
            target
        } catch (t: Throwable) {
            part.delete()
            throw t
        }
    }

    private suspend fun unpackWithJcefmaven(jar: File) = withContext(Dispatchers.IO) {
        val server = HttpServer.create(InetSocketAddress(InetAddress.getByName("127.0.0.1"), 0), 0)
        server.createContext("/") { exchange ->
            try {
                if (exchange.requestURI.path.removePrefix("/") != jar.name) {
                    exchange.sendResponseHeaders(404, -1)
                } else {
                    exchange.sendResponseHeaders(200, jar.length())
                    jar.inputStream().use { it.copyTo(exchange.responseBody) }
                }
            } finally {
                exchange.close()
            }
        }
        server.start()
        try {
            CefAppBuilder().apply {
                setInstallDir(installDir)
                setMirrors(listOf("http://127.0.0.1:${server.address.port}/jcef-natives-{platform}-{tag}.jar"))
                setProgressHandler { step, percent -> logger.d(TAG, "jcefmaven $step $percent") }
            }.install()
        } finally {
            server.stop(0)
        }
    }

    private companion object {
        const val TAG = "CefInstaller"
    }
}
```
`desktop/web/src/main/kotlin/com/example/musicfy/desktop/web/DesktopBotGuardRuntime.kt`:
```kotlin
package com.example.musicfy.desktop.web

import com.example.musicfy.core.resolver.BotGuardRuntime
import com.example.musicfy.core.resolver.BotGuardSession

class BotGuardUnavailableException : Exception("The browser component (JCEF) is not installed")

/**
 * The desktop BotGuardRuntime. [isSupported] stays true because PoTokenGenerator reads it once (by
 * lazy): a runtime that started "unsupported" could never use CEF installed later. Without CEF,
 * [newSession] asks the UI to offer the component once and throws, which PoTokenGenerator turns into
 * its cooldown while the resolver moves on to the next client (spec §2.5 #3).
 */
class DesktopBotGuardRuntime(
    private val installed: () -> Boolean,
    private val cef: () -> BotGuardRuntime,
    private val onUnavailable: () -> Unit,
) : BotGuardRuntime {
    override val isSupported: Boolean = true

    override suspend fun newSession(): BotGuardSession {
        if (!installed()) {
            onUnavailable()
            throw BotGuardUnavailableException()
        }
        return cef().newSession()
    }
}
```

`desktop/web/src/main/kotlin/com/example/musicfy/desktop/web/CefRuntime.kt`:
```kotlin
package com.example.musicfy.desktop.web

import com.example.musicfy.core.platform.Logger
import kotlinx.coroutines.Dispatchers
import kotlinx.coroutines.sync.Mutex
import kotlinx.coroutines.sync.withLock
import kotlinx.coroutines.withContext
import me.friwi.jcefmaven.CefAppBuilder
import me.friwi.jcefmaven.MavenCefAppHandlerAdapter
import org.cef.CefApp
import org.cef.CefSettings
import java.io.File

/** The one CefApp per process, created on first use from an installed bundle. */
class CefRuntime(private val installDir: File, private val dataDir: File, private val logger: Logger) {
    private val lock = Mutex()

    @Volatile
    private var app: CefApp? = null

    suspend fun app(): CefApp = app ?: lock.withLock {
        app ?: withContext(Dispatchers.IO) {
            val builder = CefAppBuilder()
            builder.setInstallDir(installDir)
            builder.setSkipInstallation(true)
            builder.cefSettings.windowless_rendering_enabled = true
            // CEF requires cache_path to be root_cache_path or inside it. The profile is the login's (D3).
            builder.cefSettings.root_cache_path = dataDir.absolutePath
            builder.cefSettings.cache_path = dataDir.resolve("profile").absolutePath
            builder.cefSettings.persist_session_cookies = true
            builder.cefSettings.log_severity = CefSettings.LogSeverity.LOGSEVERITY_WARNING
            builder.setAppHandler(object : MavenCefAppHandlerAdapter() {})
            builder.build()
        }.also {
            app = it
            logger.i(TAG, "JCEF ${CefArtifacts.VERSION} initialised")
        }
    }

    fun dispose() {
        app?.dispose()
        app = null
    }

    private companion object {
        const val TAG = "CefRuntime"
    }
}
```
