`media/MediaSessionBinder.kt`:
```kotlin
package com.example.musicfy.desktop.integration.media

import com.example.musicfy.desktop.player.controller.PlaybackStatus
import com.example.musicfy.desktop.player.controller.PlayerCommand
import com.example.musicfy.desktop.player.controller.PlayerState
import com.example.musicfy.models.MediaMetadata
import com.example.musicfy.ui.utils.resize
import kotlinx.coroutines.CoroutineScope
import kotlinx.coroutines.Job
import kotlinx.coroutines.flow.StateFlow
import kotlinx.coroutines.flow.collectLatest
import kotlinx.coroutines.flow.distinctUntilChanged
import kotlinx.coroutines.flow.map
import kotlinx.coroutines.launch
import kotlin.math.abs

/** PlayerState → OS Now Playing; OS buttons → PlayerCommand (only through dispatch, spec K18). */
class MediaSessionBinder(
    private val bridge: MediaSessionBridge,
    private val artwork: ArtworkCache,
    private val scope: CoroutineScope,
) {
    fun bind(state: StateFlow<PlayerState>, dispatch: (PlayerCommand) -> Unit): Job {
        bridge.setCommandSink { command -> dispatch(command.toPlayerCommand()) }
        return scope.launch {
            launch {
                state.map { it.current to it.durationMs }.distinctUntilChanged().collectLatest { (item, durationMs) ->
                    if (item == null) {
                        bridge.update(null)
                        return@collectLatest
                    }
                    bridge.update(item.toNowPlaying(durationMs, null))
                    bridge.setPlaybackState(state.value.status.toSessionState(), state.value.positionMs)
                    artwork.fileFor(item.id, item.thumbnailUrl?.resize(544, 544))?.let { file ->
                        bridge.update(item.toNowPlaying(durationMs, file))
                        bridge.setPlaybackState(state.value.status.toSessionState(), state.value.positionMs)
                    }
                }
            }
            launch {
                var lastState: SessionState? = null
                var lastPosition = 0L
                state.collect { s ->
                    val session = s.status.toSessionState()
                    val jumped = abs(s.positionMs - lastPosition) > SEEK_JUMP_MS
                    if (session != lastState || jumped) bridge.setPlaybackState(session, s.positionMs)
                    lastState = session
                    lastPosition = s.positionMs
                }
            }
        }
    }

    private fun MediaMetadata.toNowPlaying(durationMs: Long, file: java.io.File?) =
        NowPlaying(title, artists.joinToString { it.name }, album?.title, durationMs, file)

    private fun PlaybackStatus.toSessionState(): SessionState = when (this) {
        PlaybackStatus.Playing, PlaybackStatus.Loading -> SessionState.Playing
        PlaybackStatus.Paused -> SessionState.Paused
        PlaybackStatus.Idle, PlaybackStatus.Ended, PlaybackStatus.Error -> SessionState.Stopped
    }

    private fun MediaCommand.toPlayerCommand(): PlayerCommand = when (this) {
        MediaCommand.Play -> PlayerCommand.Play
        MediaCommand.Pause, MediaCommand.Stop -> PlayerCommand.Pause
        MediaCommand.TogglePlayPause -> PlayerCommand.TogglePlayPause
        MediaCommand.Next -> PlayerCommand.Next
        MediaCommand.Previous -> PlayerCommand.Previous
        is MediaCommand.SeekTo -> PlayerCommand.SeekTo(ms)
    }

    private companion object {
        const val SEEK_JUMP_MS = 2_000L
    }
}
```

```kotlin
package com.example.musicfy.desktop.di

import com.example.musicfy.core.platform.AppDirs
import com.example.musicfy.desktop.DeepLinkHandler
import com.example.musicfy.desktop.integration.UrlSchemeRegistrar
import com.example.musicfy.desktop.integration.media.ArtworkCache

import com.example.musicfy.desktop.integration.media.MediaSessionBridges
import com.example.musicfy.desktop.player.controller.PlayerController
import org.koin.core.module.Module
import org.koin.dsl.module

fun integrationModule(): Module = module {
    single { DeepLinkHandler(dispatch = { get<PlayerController>().dispatch(it) }, logger = get(), scope = get()) }
    single { UrlSchemeRegistrar(get()) }
    single<MediaSessionBridge> { MediaSessionBridges.create(get(), MediaSessionBridges.nativeDir()) }
    single { ArtworkCache(get<AppDirs>().cache.resolve("artwork"), get()) }
    single { MediaSessionBinder(get(), get(), get()) }
}
```

`DownloadStoreTest.kt`:
```kotlin
package com.example.musicfy.desktop.downloads

import androidx.room.Room
import androidx.sqlite.driver.bundled.BundledSQLiteDriver
import com.example.musicfy.core.platform.StdoutLogger
import com.example.musicfy.db.desktop.DesktopMusicDatabase
import com.example.musicfy.db.entities.FormatEntity
import com.example.musicfy.models.MediaMetadata
import kotlinx.coroutines.Dispatchers
import kotlinx.coroutines.runBlocking
import org.junit.After
import org.junit.Assert.assertEquals
import org.junit.Assert.assertFalse
import org.junit.Assert.assertNull
import org.junit.Assert.assertTrue
import org.junit.Rule
import org.junit.Test
import org.junit.rules.TemporaryFolder

class DownloadStoreTest {
    @get:Rule
    val tmp = TemporaryFolder()

    private val db = Room.inMemoryDatabaseBuilder<DesktopMusicDatabase>().setDriver(BundledSQLiteDriver()).setQueryCoroutineContext(Dispatchers.IO).build()
    private val song = MediaMetadata(id = "s1", title = "One", artists = emptyList(), duration = 100)
    private val format = FormatEntity("s1", 251, "audio/webm", "opus", 160_000, 48_000, 10, null, null, null)

    @After
    fun close() = db.close()

    @Test
    fun pathsFollowTheSpecLayout() {
        val paths = DownloadPaths(tmp.root)
        assertEquals(tmp.root.resolve("audio/s1.251.webm"), paths.audio("s1", 251, "audio/webm"))
        assertEquals(tmp.root.resolve("audio/s1.140.m4a.part"), paths.part("s1", 140, "audio/mp4"))
        assertEquals(tmp.root.resolve("artwork/s1.jpg"), paths.artwork("s1"))
    }

    @Test
    fun aDownloadIsPlayableOnlyWithTheRightSizeAndStartupResetsBrokenOnes() = runBlocking {
        var now = 100L * 24 * 3600 * 1000
        val store = DownloadStore(DownloadPaths(tmp.root), db.dao, StdoutLogger, now = { now })
        store.markDownloaded(song, format)
        val file = store.paths.audio("s1", 251, "audio/webm").apply { parentFile.mkdirs(); writeBytes(ByteArray(10)) }
        assertEquals(file, store.fileFor("s1"))

        file.writeBytes(ByteArray(9))
        assertNull(store.fileFor("s1"))
        val oldPart = store.paths.part("old", 251, "audio/webm").apply { writeBytes(ByteArray(1)); setLastModified(now - 8L * 24 * 3600 * 1000) }
        val freshPart = store.paths.part("fresh", 251, "audio/webm").apply { writeBytes(ByteArray(1)); setLastModified(now - 1_000) }

        assertEquals(ReconcileReport(reset = 1, deletedParts = 1), store.reconcile())
        assertEquals(false, db.dao.songEntity("s1")?.isDownloaded)
        assertFalse(oldPart.exists())
        assertTrue(freshPart.exists())
    }

    @Test
    fun deleteRemovesFileArtworkAndFlag() = runBlocking {
        val store = DownloadStore(DownloadPaths(tmp.root), db.dao, StdoutLogger)
        store.markDownloaded(song, format)
        val file = store.paths.audio("s1", 251, "audio/webm").apply { parentFile.mkdirs(); writeBytes(ByteArray(10)) }
        val art = store.paths.artwork("s1").apply { parentFile.mkdirs(); writeBytes(ByteArray(3)) }
        store.delete("s1")
        assertFalse(file.exists())
        assertFalse(art.exists())
        assertEquals(false, db.dao.songEntity("s1")?.isDownloaded)
    }
}
```

`DownloadManagerTest.kt`:
```kotlin
package com.example.musicfy.desktop.downloads

import androidx.room.Room
import androidx.sqlite.driver.bundled.BundledSQLiteDriver
import com.example.musicfy.core.platform.StdoutLogger
import com.example.musicfy.db.desktop.DesktopMusicDatabase
import com.example.musicfy.desktop.player.persist.QueueItem
import com.example.musicfy.desktop.player.stream.ResolvedStream
import com.example.musicfy.desktop.player.stream.StreamFetcher
import com.example.musicfy.desktop.player.stream.StreamResolver
import com.example.musicfy.models.MediaMetadata
import kotlinx.coroutines.CompletableDeferred
import kotlinx.coroutines.CoroutineScope
import kotlinx.coroutines.Dispatchers
import kotlinx.coroutines.SupervisorJob
import kotlinx.coroutines.cancel
import kotlinx.coroutines.delay
import kotlinx.coroutines.runBlocking
import kotlinx.coroutines.withTimeout
import kotlinx.serialization.builtins.ListSerializer
import kotlinx.serialization.json.Json
import mockwebserver3.Dispatcher
import mockwebserver3.MockResponse
import mockwebserver3.MockWebServer
import mockwebserver3.RecordedRequest
import okhttp3.OkHttpClient
import okio.Buffer
import org.junit.After
import org.junit.Assert.assertArrayEquals
import org.junit.Assert.assertEquals
import org.junit.Assert.assertFalse
import org.junit.Assert.assertTrue
import org.junit.Rule
import org.junit.Test
import org.junit.rules.TemporaryFolder
import java.util.concurrent.CopyOnWriteArrayList
import java.util.concurrent.atomic.AtomicInteger
import kotlin.random.Random

class DownloadManagerTest {
    @get:Rule
    val tmp = TemporaryFolder()

    private val size = 3 * 1024 * 1024
    private val bytes = Random(3).nextBytes(size)
    private val ranges = CopyOnWriteArrayList<String?>()

    /** 206 ranges like googlevideo; [truncateFrom] makes every range starting there return 10 bytes. */
    private var truncateFrom = Long.MAX_VALUE
    private val server = MockWebServer().apply {
        dispatcher = object : Dispatcher() {
            override fun dispatch(request: RecordedRequest): MockResponse {
                val range = request.headers["Range"]
                ranges += range
                val m = Regex("""bytes=(\d+)-(\d+)""").matchEntire(range ?: "")!!
                val start = m.groupValues[1].toLong()
                val end = minOf(m.groupValues[2].toLong(), size - 1L)
                val length = if (start >= truncateFrom) 10 else (end - start + 1).toInt()
                return MockResponse.Builder().code(206)
                    .setHeader("Content-Range", "bytes $start-$end/$size")
                    .body(Buffer().write(bytes, start.toInt(), length)).build()
            }
        }
        start()
    }
    private val db = Room.inMemoryDatabaseBuilder<DesktopMusicDatabase>().setDriver(BundledSQLiteDriver()).setQueryCoroutineContext(Dispatchers.IO).build()
    private val scope = CoroutineScope(SupervisorJob() + Dispatchers.IO)
    private val song = MediaMetadata(id = "s1", title = "One", artists = emptyList(), duration = 180)

    private val resolver = StreamResolver { id, _ ->
        ResolvedStream(id, server.url("/$id").toString(), mapOf("Origin" to "https://www.youtube.com"), "audio/webm", 251, size.toLong(), Long.MAX_VALUE, codecs = "opus", bitrate = 160_000)
    }

    @After
    fun tearDown() {
        scope.cancel()
        server.close()
        db.close()
    }

    private fun manager(resolver: StreamResolver = this.resolver, queueFile: java.io.File = tmp.root.resolve("downloads.json")) = DownloadManager(
        store = DownloadStore(DownloadPaths(tmp.root.resolve("download")), db.dao, StdoutLogger),
        fetcher = StreamFetcher(OkHttpClient(), resolver, StdoutLogger),
        resolver = resolver,
        http = OkHttpClient(),
        queueFile = queueFile,
        scope = scope,
        logger = StdoutLogger,
        chunkSize = 1024 * 1024,
    )

    private suspend fun DownloadManager.await(id: String): DownloadState = withTimeout(10_000) {
        while (true) {
            val s = states.value[id]
            if (s is DownloadState.Completed || s is DownloadState.Failed) return@withTimeout s
            delay(20)
        }
        @Suppress("UNREACHABLE_CODE") error("unreachable")
    }

    @Test
    fun aCompletedDownloadIsMovedIntoPlaceAndRecorded() = runBlocking {
        val m = manager()
        m.enqueue(song)
        assertEquals(DownloadState.Completed, m.await("s1"))
        val file = tmp.root.resolve("download/audio/s1.251.webm")
        assertArrayEquals(bytes, file.readBytes())
        assertFalse(tmp.root.resolve("download/audio/s1.251.webm.part").exists())
        assertEquals(true, db.dao.songEntity("s1")?.isDownloaded)
        assertEquals(size.toLong(), db.dao.format("s1")?.contentLength)
        assertEquals(listOf("bytes=0-1048575", "bytes=1048576-2097151", "bytes=2097152-3145727"), ranges)
    }

    @Test
    fun aPartFileResumesAtItsSize() = runBlocking {
        val part = tmp.root.resolve("download/audio/s1.251.webm.part").apply { parentFile.mkdirs(); writeBytes(bytes.copyOf(1_000_000)) }
        val m = manager()
        m.enqueue(song)
        assertEquals(DownloadState.Completed, m.await("s1"))
        assertEquals("bytes=1000000-2048575", ranges.first())
        assertArrayEquals(bytes, tmp.root.resolve("download/audio/s1.251.webm").readBytes())
        assertFalse(part.exists())
    }

    @Test
    fun aShortReadFailsAndKeepsThePartForResume() = runBlocking {
        truncateFrom = 1024L * 1024
        val m = manager()
        m.enqueue(song)
        val state = m.await("s1")
        assertTrue("got $state", state is DownloadState.Failed && state.reason.contains("short read"))
        assertTrue(tmp.root.resolve("download/audio/s1.251.webm.part").exists())
        assertFalse(tmp.root.resolve("download/audio/s1.251.webm").exists())
    }

    @Test
    fun pendingDownloadsSurviveARestart() = runBlocking {
        val queueFile = tmp.root.resolve("downloads.json")
        queueFile.writeText(Json.encodeToString(ListSerializer(QueueItem.serializer()), listOf(QueueItem(id = "s1", title = "One", duration = 180))))
        val m = manager(queueFile = queueFile)
        m.restore()
        assertEquals(DownloadState.Completed, m.await("s1"))
        assertEquals("[]", queueFile.readText().trim())
    }

    @Test
    fun atMostTwoRunAtOnce() = runBlocking {
        val running = AtomicInteger()
        val maxRunning = AtomicInteger()
        val gate = CompletableDeferred<Unit>()
        val slow = StreamResolver { id, bypass ->
            maxRunning.accumulateAndGet(running.incrementAndGet(), ::maxOf)
            gate.await()
            running.decrementAndGet()
            resolver.resolve(id, bypass)
        }
        val m = manager(resolver = slow)
        listOf("a", "b", "c").forEach { m.enqueue(song.copy(id = it)) }
        delay(300)
        assertEquals(2, maxRunning.get())
        gate.complete(Unit)
        listOf("a", "b", "c").forEach { assertEquals(DownloadState.Completed, m.await(it)) }
        assertEquals(2, maxRunning.get())
    }
}
```

- [ ] **Step 3: Implement**

`DownloadPaths.kt`:
```kotlin
package com.example.musicfy.desktop.downloads

import java.io.File

/**
 * Spec §2.7 layout under DesktopAppDirs.downloads. The file name derives from videoId + itag + mime
 * type, which the DB already stores (SongEntity + FormatEntity): schema v39 is unchanged.
 */
class DownloadPaths(val root: File) {
    val audioDir: File = root.resolve("audio")
    val artworkDir: File = root.resolve("artwork")

    fun audio(videoId: String, itag: Int, mimeType: String): File = audioDir.resolve("$videoId.$itag.${extensionFor(mimeType)}")

    fun part(videoId: String, itag: Int, mimeType: String): File = File(audio(videoId, itag, mimeType).path + ".part")

    fun artwork(videoId: String): File = artworkDir.resolve("$videoId.jpg")

    companion object {
        fun extensionFor(mimeType: String): String = when (mimeType.substringBefore(';').trim().lowercase()) {
            "audio/webm" -> "webm"
            "audio/mp4" -> "m4a"
            else -> "bin"
        }
    }
}
```
`DownloadStore.kt`:
```kotlin
package com.example.musicfy.desktop.downloads

import com.example.musicfy.core.platform.Logger
import com.example.musicfy.db.desktop.DesktopDao
import com.example.musicfy.db.desktop.insert
import com.example.musicfy.db.entities.FormatEntity
import com.example.musicfy.desktop.player.stream.LocalFileLookup
import com.example.musicfy.models.MediaMetadata
import kotlinx.coroutines.flow.first
import java.io.File
import java.time.LocalDateTime

data class ReconcileReport(val reset: Int, val deletedParts: Int)

/** Which songs are downloaded, in the DB and on disk (spec §2.7). */
class DownloadStore(
    val paths: DownloadPaths,
    private val dao: DesktopDao,
    private val logger: Logger,
    private val now: () -> Long = System::currentTimeMillis,
) : LocalFileLookup {
    /** The complete file for a downloaded song, or null (not downloaded, missing, or wrong size). */
    override suspend fun fileFor(videoId: String): File? {
        val song = dao.songEntity(videoId) ?: return null
        if (!song.isDownloaded) return null
        val format = dao.format(videoId) ?: return null
        return paths.audio(videoId, format.itag, format.mimeType).takeIf { it.isFile && it.length() == format.contentLength }
    }

    suspend fun markDownloaded(item: MediaMetadata, format: FormatEntity, at: LocalDateTime = LocalDateTime.now()) {
        dao.insert(item)
        dao.upsert(format)
        dao.updateDownloadedInfo(item.id, true, at)
    }

    suspend fun delete(videoId: String) {
        dao.format(videoId)?.let { paths.audio(videoId, it.itag, it.mimeType).delete() }
        paths.artwork(videoId).delete()
        dao.updateDownloadedInfo(videoId, false, null)
    }

    /** At start-up: un-flag downloads whose file is gone or wrong, and delete .part files older than 7 days. */
    suspend fun reconcile(): ReconcileReport {
        var reset = 0
        dao.downloadedSongs().first().forEach { s ->
            if (fileFor(s.song.id) == null) {
                dao.updateDownloadedInfo(s.song.id, false, null)
                logger.w(TAG, "download of ${s.song.id} is missing or has the wrong size; marked not downloaded")
                reset++
            }
        }
        val cutoff = now() - ORPHAN_PART_AGE_MS
        var deleted = 0
        paths.audioDir.listFiles { f -> f.name.endsWith(".part") && f.lastModified() < cutoff }?.forEach { if (it.delete()) deleted++ }
        return ReconcileReport(reset, deleted)
    }

    private companion object {
        const val TAG = "DownloadStore"
        const val ORPHAN_PART_AGE_MS = 7L * 24 * 3600 * 1000
    }
}
```
`DownloadManager.kt`:
```kotlin
package com.example.musicfy.desktop.downloads

import com.example.musicfy.core.platform.Logger
import com.example.musicfy.desktop.player.persist.QueueItem
import com.example.musicfy.desktop.player.persist.toFormatEntity
import com.example.musicfy.desktop.player.persist.toMediaMetadata
import com.example.musicfy.desktop.player.persist.toQueueItem
import com.example.musicfy.desktop.player.stream.StreamFetcher
import com.example.musicfy.desktop.player.stream.StreamResolver
import com.example.musicfy.models.MediaMetadata
import com.example.musicfy.ui.utils.resize
import kotlinx.coroutines.CancellationException
import kotlinx.coroutines.CoroutineScope
import kotlinx.coroutines.Dispatchers
import kotlinx.coroutines.Job
import kotlinx.coroutines.flow.MutableStateFlow
import kotlinx.coroutines.flow.StateFlow
import kotlinx.coroutines.flow.asStateFlow
import kotlinx.coroutines.flow.update
import kotlinx.coroutines.launch
import kotlinx.coroutines.sync.Semaphore
import kotlinx.coroutines.sync.withPermit
import kotlinx.coroutines.withContext
import kotlinx.serialization.builtins.ListSerializer
import kotlinx.serialization.json.Json
import okhttp3.OkHttpClient
import okhttp3.Request
import java.io.File
import java.io.FileOutputStream
import java.io.FilterOutputStream
import java.io.IOException
import java.io.OutputStream
import java.nio.file.Files
import java.nio.file.StandardCopyOption
import java.util.concurrent.ConcurrentHashMap

sealed interface DownloadState {
    data object Queued : DownloadState
    data class Downloading(val fraction: Float) : DownloadState
    data object Completed : DownloadState
    data class Failed(val reason: String) : DownloadState
}

/**
 * Spec §2.7: files as served (webm/opus or m4a, no remux), 4 MiB ranges through the shared
 * StreamFetcher (re-resolve on expiry), resume from the .part size, size check, ATOMIC_MOVE.
 * The pending list lives in [queueFile], so a killed app resumes on the next start.
 */
class DownloadManager(
    private val store: DownloadStore,
    private val fetcher: StreamFetcher,
    private val resolver: StreamResolver,
    private val http: OkHttpClient,
    private val queueFile: File,
    private val scope: CoroutineScope,
    private val logger: Logger,
    parallel: Int = 2,
    private val chunkSize: Long = CHUNK_SIZE,
) {
    private val _states = MutableStateFlow<Map<String, DownloadState>>(emptyMap())
    val states: StateFlow<Map<String, DownloadState>> = _states.asStateFlow()

    private val semaphore = Semaphore(parallel)
    private val jobs = ConcurrentHashMap<String, Job>()
    private val pending = ConcurrentHashMap<String, MediaMetadata>()
    private val json = Json { ignoreUnknownKeys = true }
    private val serializer = ListSerializer(QueueItem.serializer())

    fun restore() {
        val items = runCatching { if (queueFile.isFile) json.decodeFromString(serializer, queueFile.readText()) else emptyList() }
            .onFailure { logger.w(TAG, "ignoring unreadable $queueFile", it) }
            .getOrDefault(emptyList())
        items.map { it.toMediaMetadata() }.forEach(::enqueue)
    }

    fun enqueue(item: MediaMetadata) {
        if (jobs[item.id]?.isActive == true) return
        pending[item.id] = item
        saveQueue()
        setState(item.id, DownloadState.Queued)
        jobs[item.id] = scope.launch { semaphore.withPermit { run(item) } }
    }

    fun cancel(videoId: String) {
        jobs.remove(videoId)?.cancel()
        pending.remove(videoId)
        saveQueue()
        _states.update { it - videoId }
    }

    private suspend fun run(item: MediaMetadata) {
        try {
            val stream = resolver.resolve(item.id, bypassCache = false)
            val target = store.paths.audio(item.id, stream.itag, stream.mimeType)
            val part = store.paths.part(item.id, stream.itag, stream.mimeType)
            target.parentFile.mkdirs()
            val offset = if (part.isFile) part.length() else 0L
            val known = stream.contentLength
            val total = if (known != null && offset >= known) {
                known
            } else {
                val range = fetcher.open(item.id, offset, null, chunkSize)
                range.use { r ->
                    withContext(Dispatchers.IO) {
                        FileOutputStream(part, true).use { file ->
                            r.writeTo(ProgressStream(file, offset) { written -> setState(item.id, DownloadState.Downloading(written.toFloat() / r.total)) })
                        }
                    }
                    r.total
                }
            }
            if (part.length() != total) throw IOException("downloaded ${part.length()} bytes, expected $total")
            Files.move(part.toPath(), target.toPath(), StandardCopyOption.ATOMIC_MOVE, StandardCopyOption.REPLACE_EXISTING)
            saveArtwork(item)
            store.markDownloaded(item, stream.copy(contentLength = total).toFormatEntity())
            finished(item.id, DownloadState.Completed)
            logger.i(TAG, "downloaded ${item.id} (${stream.itag}, $total bytes)")
        } catch (e: CancellationException) {
            throw e // stays pending: resumes on the next start unless cancel() removed it
        } catch (e: Exception) {
            logger.w(TAG, "download of ${item.id} failed", e)
            finished(item.id, DownloadState.Failed(e.message ?: e.javaClass.simpleName))
        }
    }

    private fun finished(videoId: String, state: DownloadState) {
        pending.remove(videoId)
        saveQueue()
        setState(videoId, state)
    }

    private suspend fun saveArtwork(item: MediaMetadata) = withContext(Dispatchers.IO) {
        val url = item.thumbnailUrl?.resize(544, 544) ?: return@withContext
        runCatching {
            http.newCall(Request.Builder().url(url).build()).execute().use { r ->
                if (r.isSuccessful) {
                    val file = store.paths.artwork(item.id).apply { parentFile.mkdirs() }
                    file.outputStream().use { r.body.byteStream().copyTo(it) }
                }
            }
        }.onFailure { logger.w(TAG, "artwork for ${item.id} not saved", it) }
    }

    @Synchronized
    private fun saveQueue() {
        runCatching {
            queueFile.parentFile?.mkdirs()
            val tmp = File(queueFile.path + ".tmp")
            tmp.writeText(json.encodeToString(serializer, pending.values.map { it.toQueueItem() }))
            Files.move(tmp.toPath(), queueFile.toPath(), StandardCopyOption.REPLACE_EXISTING, StandardCopyOption.ATOMIC_MOVE)
        }.onFailure { logger.w(TAG, "could not save $queueFile", it) }
    }

    private fun setState(videoId: String, state: DownloadState) = _states.update { it + (videoId to state) }

    /** Counts bytes and reports progress at most every 256 KiB. */
    private class ProgressStream(out: OutputStream, private var written: Long, private val onProgress: (Long) -> Unit) : FilterOutputStream(out) {
        private var lastReport = written

        override fun write(b: ByteArray, off: Int, len: Int) {
            out.write(b, off, len)
            written += len
            if (written - lastReport >= 256 * 1024) {
                lastReport = written
                onProgress(written)
            }
        }

        override fun write(b: Int) {
            out.write(b)
            written++
        }
    }

    companion object {
        /** Under the ~10 MB throttle threshold yt-dlp documents (spec §2.2, R10). */
        const val CHUNK_SIZE = 4L * 1024 * 1024
        private const val TAG = "DownloadManager"
    }
}
```

```kotlin
package com.example.musicfy.desktop.di

import com.example.musicfy.core.platform.DesktopAppDirs
import com.example.musicfy.desktop.downloads.DownloadManager
import com.example.musicfy.desktop.downloads.DownloadPaths

import com.example.musicfy.desktop.player.stream.LocalFileLookup
import org.koin.core.module.Module
import org.koin.dsl.module

fun downloadsModule(): Module = module {
    single { DownloadPaths(get<DesktopAppDirs>().downloads) }
    single { DownloadStore(get(), get(), get()) }
    single<LocalFileLookup> { get<DownloadStore>() }
    single {
        DownloadManager(
            store = get(),
            fetcher = get(),
            resolver = get(),
            http = get(),
            queueFile = get<DesktopAppDirs>().localData.resolve("downloads.json"),
            scope = get(),
            logger = get(),
        )
    }
}
```

```kotlin
package com.example.musicfy.desktop.ui

import androidx.compose.foundation.layout.Column
import androidx.compose.material3.MaterialTheme
import androidx.compose.material3.OutlinedButton
import androidx.compose.material3.Text
import androidx.compose.runtime.Composable
import androidx.compose.runtime.collectAsState
import androidx.compose.runtime.getValue
import com.example.musicfy.desktop.downloads.DownloadManager
import com.example.musicfy.desktop.downloads.DownloadState
import com.example.musicfy.desktop.player.controller.PlayerController

@Composable
fun DownloadDebugSection(manager: DownloadManager, controller: PlayerController) {
    val states by manager.states.collectAsState()
    val player by controller.state.collectAsState()
    Column {
        Text("Downloads", style = MaterialTheme.typography.titleMedium)
        OutlinedButton(onClick = { player.current?.let(manager::enqueue) }, enabled = player.current != null) { Text("Download current song") }
        states.forEach { (id, state) ->
            val text = when (state) {
                DownloadState.Queued -> "queued"
                is DownloadState.Downloading -> "${(state.fraction * 100).toInt()}%"
                DownloadState.Completed -> "done"
                is DownloadState.Failed -> "failed: ${state.reason}"
            }
            Text("$id: $text", style = MaterialTheme.typography.bodySmall)
        }
    }
}
```

**Files:**

**Interfaces:**
- Consumes: every Gradle task above (`:core:playback:test`, `:desktop:{player,js,web,integration,downloads}:test`, `:desktop:player:integrationTest`, `:desktop:integration:buildNativeHelpers`, `:desktop:web:e3Probe`, `:desktopApp:test`).
- Produces: `scripts/desktop/check-imports.sh` (exit 1 on a boundary violation); the `Desktop` workflow with jobs `desktop-test` (4 runners) and `cef-e3` (manual).

- [ ] **Step 1: Boundary check script**

`scripts/desktop/check-imports.sh`:
```bash
#!/usr/bin/env bash
# Spec §1.2: which modules may use which native libraries, and which desktop modules may depend on which.
set -euo pipefail
cd "$(dirname "$0")/../.."
fail=0

# check <import regex> <allowed directory regex> <message>
check() {
  local hits
  hits="$(grep -rlE "$1" --include='*.kt' app core desktop desktopApp 2>/dev/null | grep -vE "^($2)/" || true)"
  if [ -n "$hits" ]; then
    echo "::error::$3"
    echo "$hits"
    fail=1
  fi
}
check '^import uk\.co\.caprica\.vlcj' 'desktop/player' 'vlcj may only be imported by desktop:player'
check '^import (org\.cef|me\.friwi)' 'desktop/web' 'JCEF may only be imported by desktop:web'
check '^import (com\.sun\.jna|io\.github\.selemba1000)' 'desktop/player|desktop/integration' 'JNA/JMTC may only be imported by desktop:player and desktop:integration'
check '^import (java\.awt|javax\.swing)' 'desktop/web|desktop/integration|desktopApp' 'AWT/Swing may not be used by core or desktop:player/downloads/js'

# desktop/* main dependencies on other desktop modules: only downloads->player and integration->player
for f in desktop/*/build.gradle.kts; do
  mod="$(basename "$(dirname "$f")")"
  for dep in $(grep -E '^\s*(api|implementation)\(project\(":desktop:' "$f" | sed -E 's/.*":desktop:([a-z]+)".*/\1/'); do
    case "$mod:$dep" in
      downloads:player|integration:player) ;;
      *) echo "::error::desktop:$mod must not depend on desktop:$dep (spec §1.2)"; fail=1 ;;
    esac
  done
done

if grep -qE 'project\(":desktop' app/build.gradle.kts core/*/build.gradle.kts 2>/dev/null; then
  echo "::error::app/ and core/* must not depend on desktop/* modules"
  grep -nE 'project\(":desktop' app/build.gradle.kts core/*/build.gradle.kts
  fail=1
fi

[ "$fail" = 0 ] && echo "check-imports: OK"
exit "$fail"
```
Run: `chmod +x scripts/desktop/check-imports.sh && scripts/desktop/check-imports.sh`
Expected: `check-imports: OK`. (Test-scope `testImplementation(project(":desktop:js"))` in `desktop/web` is allowed: the regex only matches `api`/`implementation`.)

- [ ] **Step 2: Final workflow**

`.github/workflows/desktop.yml` (full replacement; the `engine` job becomes `desktop-test` and runs every desktop module):
```yaml
name: Desktop

on:
  push:
    branches: [ "main", "feature/desktop-*" ]
    paths: [ "core/**", "desktop/**", "desktopApp/**", "providers/**", "scripts/desktop/**", "gradle/**", "*.gradle.kts", ".github/workflows/desktop.yml" ]
  pull_request:
    paths: [ "core/**", "desktop/**", "desktopApp/**", "providers/**", "scripts/desktop/**", "gradle/**", "*.gradle.kts", ".github/workflows/desktop.yml" ]
  workflow_dispatch:

concurrency:
  group: desktop-${{ github.ref }}
  cancel-in-progress: true

jobs:
  desktop-test:
    name: Desktop tests (${{ matrix.os }})
    runs-on: ${{ matrix.os }}
    strategy:
      fail-fast: false
      matrix:
        os: [ ubuntu-latest, macos-15, macos-15-intel, windows-latest ]
    defaults:
      run:
        shell: bash
    steps:
      - uses: actions/checkout@v6
        with:
          submodules: recursive

      - name: Set up JDK 21
        uses: actions/setup-java@v5
        with:
          java-version: 21
          distribution: "temurin"

      - name: Set Up Gradle
        uses: gradle/actions/setup-gradle@v5
        with:
          cache-read-only: ${{ github.ref != 'refs/heads/main' }}

      - name: Module boundaries (spec §1.2)
        if: runner.os == 'Linux'
        run: scripts/desktop/check-imports.sh

      - name: libVLC from the system (Linux)
        if: runner.os == 'Linux'
        run: sudo apt-get update && sudo apt-get install -y libvlc-dev vlc-plugin-base

      - name: Bundled libVLC 3.0.23
        if: runner.os != 'Linux'
        run: scripts/desktop/fetch-libvlc.sh

      - name: Native helpers (macOS)
        if: runner.os == 'macOS'
        run: ./gradlew --console=plain :desktop:integration:buildNativeHelpers

      - name: Unit + headless engine tests
        run: >
          ./gradlew --console=plain
          :core:playback:test
          :desktop:player:test :desktop:player:integrationTest
          :desktop:js:test :desktop:web:test :desktop:integration:test :desktop:downloads:test
          :desktopApp:test

      - uses: actions/upload-artifact@v6
        if: failure()
        with:
          name: desktop-test-reports-${{ matrix.os }}
          path: |
            core/playback/build/reports/tests
            desktop/*/build/reports/tests
            desktopApp/build/reports/tests

  cef-e3:
    name: E3 JCEF BotGuard (${{ matrix.os }})
    if: github.event_name == 'workflow_dispatch'
    runs-on: ${{ matrix.os }}
    strategy:
      fail-fast: false
      matrix:
        os: [ macos-15, macos-15-intel, windows-latest ]
    defaults:
      run:
        shell: bash
    steps:
      - uses: actions/checkout@v6
        with:
          submodules: recursive
      - name: Set up JDK 21
        uses: actions/setup-java@v5
        with:
          java-version: 21
          distribution: "temurin"
      - name: Set Up Gradle
        uses: gradle/actions/setup-gradle@v5
        with:
          cache-read-only: true
      - name: E3 probe (off-screen)
        run: ./gradlew --console=plain :desktop:web:e3Probe
```
